Method: 采用混合利益相关者研讨会形式，召集30名社区代表、警察和学者，共同评估13个警务AI用例的风险，明确聚焦种族偏见。通过讨论和投票决定哪些用例可接受。

Result: 参与者总体上对AI持开放态度，仅拒绝了三个用例，其中最显著的是累犯风险评估工具（反对其前提而非具体实施）。讨论并未因聚焦种族公正而变得狭隘，而是转向三个核心问题：工具是否有效、能否带来真正益处、益处是否惠及所有人。

Conclusion: 将种族偏见视角从一开始就纳入AI用例的风险-收益分析，有助于引发更根本的、惠及所有人的讨论（类似包容性设计的路缘效应），从而提升决策质量。

Abstract: AI tools are being increasingly adopted in policing in the UK and worldwide. Racial bias is a known and well-documented risk, yet representatives of affected communities are rarely included in decisions about AI adoption. We present results from a mixed-stakeholder deliberation workshop bringing together 30 community representatives, police officers, and academics to assess the risks of 13 AI use cases in policing, with an explicit focus on racial bias. We found that participants were broadly open to AI adoption, rejecting only three use cases outright, most notably recidivism risk assessment, where objections targeted the premise rather than the implementation. Our analysis reveals that foregrounding racial equity did not narrow the deliberation. Instead, discussions gravitated toward a fundamental set of questions: does this tool actually work, will it deliver genuine benefit, and will that benefit extend to everyone? This integrated reasoning, reminiscent of the curb-cut effect in inclusive design, highlights the benefit of incorporating the racial bias lens into the risk-benefit analysis of AI use cases from the outset.

</details>


### [200] [SCP-NL2TL: Selective Conformal Prediction with Semantic Verification for Natural Language to Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05439)
*Yixuan Wang,Licheng Luo,Yu Fu,Kaidi Xu,Yue Dong,Mingyu Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种选择性自然语言到形式规范的翻译框架，通过结合反向翻译保真度和语义等价重复翻译离散度两种信号，利用共形风险控制校准决策以决定接受或放弃规范生成，并在不同形式语言（STL、LTL、SpaTiaL）上验证了翻译可靠性和不确定性感知的可靠性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将自然语言指令翻译为形式规范（如时序逻辑）的模型会为所有输入生成规范，即使结果不可靠或未捕获用户意图，这在安全关键应用中存在风险。因此需要一种能够评估翻译可靠性并在不可靠时主动放弃翻译的机制。

Method: 提出选择性翻译框架：使用两种互补的黑箱信号（规范反向翻译回自然语言的保真度、在精确语义等价下重复翻译的离散度）评估可靠性，然后通过共形风险控制（conformal risk control）校准该分数，形成接受或放弃的决策，并给出无分布界定的错误接受率上限；同时使用共形异常检测器在翻译前过滤分布外（OOD）输入。该框架通用，适用于多种形式规范语言。

Result: 在信号时序逻辑（STL）、线性时序逻辑（LTL）和几何时空逻辑（SpaTiaL）上的实验表明，该方法提高了翻译可靠性，在评估的跨层偏移（cross-tier shifts）下具有鲁棒性，并实现了有效的不确定性感知放弃（abstention）。

Conclusion: 该工作为可信赖的自然语言接口奠定了基础，使得AI系统能够识别何时生成的规范可能不可靠，从而在安全关键应用中避免风险。

Abstract: Translating natural language instructions into machine-interpretable formal specifications enables robots and autonomous systems to plan, reason, and formally verify their behavior. However, existing translation models typically generate a specification for every input, even when the result is unreliable or fails to capture the user's intent, creating risks in safety-critical applications. Inspired by selective conformal prediction, we propose a selective translation framework that not only generates formal specifications but also determines when they can be trusted. Reliability is scored by two complementary black-box signals, the fidelity of the specification back-translated into natural language and the dispersion of repeated translations under exact semantic equivalence, which fail on different errors and jointly separate incorrect translations more sharply than either alone. Conformal risk control calibrates this score into a decision that accepts a specification or abstains, with a distribution-free bound on the rate at which incorrect specifications are accepted for execution, and a conformal anomaly detector on instruction embeddings screens out-of-distribution inputs before any translation is attempted. The proposed framework is general across formal specification languages, with experiments on Signal Temporal Logic (STL), Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), and geometric Spatio-Temporal Logic (SpaTiaL) demonstrating improved translation reliability, robustness under the evaluated cross-tier shifts, and effective uncertainty-aware abstention. This work establishes a foundation for trustworthy natural language interfaces by enabling AI systems to recognize when generated specifications may not be reliable.

</details>


### [201] [Stochasticity Is Not the Hard Part: Reduction and Complexity in Instructional Sequencing over Prerequisite DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05455)
*Zonglin Han,Yichen Chen,Jiawen Jiang,Tongan Shi,Kristian A. Stevens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper studies instructional sequencing as a stochastic shortest-path problem, proves it collapses to a deterministic problem but remains NP-hard, identifies tractable cases when transfer preferences are acyclic with prerequisites, and provides a diagnostic to bound the value of sequencing; experiments on real course data show a "doubly easy" regime while constructed instances show hardness where A* with consistent heuristic is efficient.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understand when the order of instruction matters in learning concepts with prerequisite dependencies, and what is the computational cost of finding the optimal sequence.

Method: Models instructional sequencing as a stochastic shortest-path problem; proves stochasticity can be eliminated exactly, reducing to a deterministic shortest-path problem on the lattice of prerequisite order ideals; shows NP-hardness via reduction from feedback arc set in tournaments; identifies acyclic case where topological order is optimal; introduces a computable diagnostic mΔ to bound the value of sequencing.

Result: Stochasticity can be eliminated but optimal sequencing remains NP-hard even under severe restrictions; hardness is not uniform: if transfer preferences are jointly acyclic with prerequisites, any topological order is optimal and fixed prerequisite width yields polynomial-time DP. The diagnostic mΔ certifies a "doubly easy" regime on real course data (70,893 interactions) with little value to optimize and little space to search, while constructed instances realize the challenging regime where myopic sequencing suffers large regret yet A* with consistent heuristic expands only linearly many states.

Conclusion: Instructional sequencing is computationally hard in general, but the hardness boundary is characterized, and a diagnostic can identify easy regimes where optimization is unnecessary or tractable; even in hard regimes, A* with a consistent heuristic can be efficient.

Abstract: When a student must learn concepts connected by prerequisite dependencies, when does the order of instruction matter, and what does it cost to find the best one? We study instructional sequencing as a stochastic shortest-path problem in which attempting a concept succeeds with a state-dependent probability and failure leaves the learner state unchanged. We first prove that this stochasticity can be eliminated exactly: the problem collapses to a deterministic shortest-path problem on the lattice of prerequisite order ideals, preserving optimal values and actions. The collapse removes stochastic complexity but not combinatorial complexity: optimal sequencing remains NP-hard -- via reduction from feedback arc set in tournaments -- even with no prerequisite edges, unit costs, uniform binary nonnegative transfer, and success probabilities at least $1/2$. Hardness is not uniform: when realizable transfer preferences remain jointly acyclic with the prerequisites, any topological order of the residual joint graph is optimal, and fixed prerequisite width yields polynomial-time exact dynamic programming. A computable diagnostic, $mΔ$, bounds the value of sequencing before optimization. On 70,893 interactions from an introductory CS course, the diagnostic certifies a doubly easy regime -- little value to optimize and little space to search -- while constructed transfer instances realize the challenging regime, where myopic sequencing suffers large regret yet exact A* with a consistent heuristic expands only linearly many states on that family.

</details>


### [202] [Recursive Synthesis for Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05466)
*Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Ruhan Wang,Anhao Li,Zixun Huang,Junyao Yang,Lei Ke,Ninghao Liu,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了递归式合成终端任务（RST）框架，通过递归验证合成方式，以每任务约$0.05的成本生成37,484个长程终端代理任务，任务难度随递归轮次显著增加（DeepSeek-V4-Pro pass@4从90%降至2.5%），并且在这些合成任务上微调Qwen3.5模型在三个终端基准测试上取得了最高10个点的提升，agentic PPO进一步带来20-41%的相对收益。递归过程在15轮后未见天花板，表明可以持续扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的长程终端代理训练数据成本极高（每任务数百至数千美元），人工构建无法规模化，且LLM直接生成会破坏指令、环境、参考解和验证器之间的依赖一致性。

Method: 提出递归式合成终端任务（RST）框架：从已验证的种子任务出发，递归地扩展参考方案、重新对齐验证器和指令到新的工作流、在新沙箱中验证结果，并将接受的任务作为后续轮次的种子，循环迭代15轮。

Result: RST以约$0.05/任务的成本生成了37,484个合成终端代理任务；任务难度随轮次递增（中位参考方案从67行增至374行，中位执行命令从40增至244，DeepSeek-V4-Pro pass@4从90%降至2.5%）；在合成任务上收集Qwen3.5轨迹进行SFT，使Qwen3.5-27B和122B模型在三个终端基准上提升最多10个点；agentic PPO使Qwen3.5-27B分别达到49.44%、32.00%和22.07%，相对增益20-41%。递归15轮后未见天花板。

Conclusion: RST是一种低成本、可扩展的高质量长程终端代理任务合成方法，能够生成难度递增的任务用于有效训练终端代理模型，且递归过程可持续扩展。

Abstract: High-quality long-horizon training data for terminal agents is expensive to produce, often costing hundreds to thousands of dollars per task, because each task must keep the instruction, environment, reference solution, and verifier mutually consistent. Human authoring does not scale, and direct generation with large language models (LLMs) often breaks these dependencies. We present Recursive Synthetic Terminal Tasks (RST), a recursive verified synthesis framework for constructing long-horizon terminal-agent tasks at scale. Starting from verified seed tasks, RST extends the reference solution, realigns the verifier and instruction to the new workflow, validates the result in a fresh sandbox, and reuses accepted tasks as seeds for subsequent rounds. Across fifteen recursive rounds, RST produces 37,484 synthesized terminal-agent tasks at roughly \$0.05 per task. Task difficulty increases substantially over rounds: the median reference solution grows from 67 to 374 lines, the median number of executed commands grows from 40 to 244, and DeepSeek-V4-Pro pass@4 drops from 90\% at $R_1$ to 2.5\% at $R_{15}$. To demonstrate training utility, we collect rejection-sampled Qwen3.5 trajectories on the synthesized tasks and use them for supervised fine-tuning. Fine-tuning on these trajectories improves Qwen3.5-27B and Qwen3.5-122B-A10B by up to 10 points on Terminal-Bench~2, Terminal-Bench Hard, and Long-Horizon Terminal Bench, while agentic PPO lifts Qwen3.5-27B to 49.44\%, 32.00\%, and 22.07\% on the three benchmarks, corresponding to relative gains of 20.0\%, 41.2\%, and 21.9\% over the base model. Moreover, after 15 rounds, the recursion shows no ceiling: synthesis yield and validation rates remain stable as difficulty keeps climbing, indicating that the process can continue well beyond the scale reported here.

</details>


### [203] [Innovation-Residual Auditing of Autonomous Analysis Agents: Localization, Detection Limits, Error Control, and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05490)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主数据分析代理的审计可靠性：当分析出错时，如何定位导致错误的操作。现有方法利用已知正确的分析训练模型，通过标记偏离预测的操作来定位错误，但尚未系统评估其可靠性。本文分析了评分方式对错误定位的影响，提出了控制虚警率的程序，并揭示了错误可检测性的基本极限——误差幅度低于一定阈值时无法与正确分析中的正常变异区分，且该极限随正确分析样本数增加而下降极慢，因此表征维度而非训练数据量是主要约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理可独立完成数据分析流程，但一旦结果出错，需要定位具体操作错误。现有无监督审计方法通过学习正确分析的模式来标记异常操作，但其可靠性未被研究。

Method: 本文首先分析评分函数的选择：若仅根据前一步操作计算意外程度，则继承性错误无法区分，一次错误只产生一个标记；若基于更长上下文重建预期分析，则错误会扩散到多个操作。量化了扩散程度及误差累积时的比较长度选择。接着提出了控制单次审计中虚警比例的程序，仅需正确分析可交换而非模型正确，并量化了模型不完美或选择偏差时保证的削弱程度。最后建立了审计可报告的错误下限：低于一定幅度的错误无法归因，与正确分析间的正常变异无异。该下限随正确分析样本数增加而下降极慢，百倍样本仅减少不到2%，因此表征维度是瓶颈。

Result: 量化了不同评分策略下错误定位的表现：短上下文导致一次错误一个标记，长上下文导致错误扩散。给出了控制虚警率的程序和理论保证。揭示了错误可检测性的基本极限，并指出表征维度比训练数据量更关键。

Conclusion: 审计可靠性受限于表征维度而非数据量；错误幅度低于一定水平则不可归因。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now carry out entire data analyses, selecting cohorts, joining tables, and fitting models with little step-by-step supervision. When such an analysis turns out to be wrong, someone must determine which operation caused it. A recent approach does this without any labelled mistakes, learning instead from analyses known to be sound and flagging operations that depart from what that model predicts; how reliable such audits are has not been studied. This paper supplies that analysis. The choice of score determines whether an error can be localized at all. If each operation is scored by how surprising it is given the operation immediately preceding it, then operations that merely inherit an earlier error are indistinguishable from correct ones, so one mistake produces one flag; scores computed against a longer reconstruction of the intended analysis instead spread a single mistake across many operations. We quantify how far they spread, and how to choose the comparison length when an error accumulates gradually rather than at once. We then give procedures that control the proportion of falsely flagged operations within a single audited analysis, requiring only that sound analyses be exchangeable rather than that the fitted model be correct, and we quantify how much the guarantees weaken when the model is imperfect or when the analysis was selected for review in a way that depends on its content. Finally we establish a limit on what any such audit can report: errors below a certain magnitude cannot be attributed at all, being indistinguishable from ordinary variation among sound analyses. This limit falls so slowly as more sound analyses are collected that at the representation sizes now in use a hundredfold increase reduces it by under two percent, so the dimension of the representation rather than the volume of training data is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [204] [EcoAgent-Bench: Evaluating Economic Decision-Making in Budget-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05519)
*Jie Wu,Ming Gong,Feixiang Cheng,Qinqin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EcoAgent-Bench 是一个评估语言代理在预算约束下经济行动选择的基准，包含 304 个真实衍生任务，测试代理在避免不必要升级、证据不足时升级、选择模型层级和停止无依据前提四类决策。实验显示，工具-API 代理的微观严格成功率仅 3.9-24.0%，经济一致性最高仅 7.3%，说明完成任务与成本效益行动选择是不同特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常只衡量任务完成度，将资源使用视为辅助统计量；然而在实际部署中，如何在本地查询、广泛搜索、复合研究工具、更强模型或人工升级之间做出选择本身就是任务的一部分。因此需要一种能够显式评估代理在经济约束下行动选择的基准。

Method: 构建 EcoAgent-Bench，每个任务指定定价动作和明确预算；包含 304 个从 GAIA、HotpotQA 和 MuSiQue 改编的真实任务，涵盖五个家族，测试四类决策。在工具-API 和工作区-CLI 两种设置下评估七个 LLM 代理和四个脚本化控制。除了微观平均准确率，还提出经济一致性分数（升级导向和节省导向家族组中准确率的较差值）以暴露单边策略的失败。

Result: 工具-API 代理的微观严格成功率为 3.9-24.0%，经济一致性最高仅 7.3%。始终升级的控制获得高微观成功但节省导向任务失败。阈值扫描将 GPT-5.4 的升级率从 0% 改变为仅 3%。

Conclusion: 在预算下完成任务与经济行动选择是两种不同属性；现有代理在预算约束下的经济决策表现不佳，需要更好的方法来实现成本效益的行动选择。基准和工具已开源以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks usually measure task completion and treat resource use as an auxiliary statistic. In deployment, however, the choice among a local lookup, broad search, composite research tool, stronger model, or human escalation is part of the task itself. We introduce EcoAgent-Bench, in which every task specifies priced actions and an explicit budget. Its 304 real-derived tasks span five families adapted from GAIA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, and test four decisions: avoiding unnecessary escalation, escalating when local evidence is insufficient, selecting a model tier, and stopping on unsupported premises. We evaluate seven LLM agents in tool-API and workspace-CLI settings, together with four oracle scripted controls. Micro-averaged accuracy rewards one-sided policies: always-escalate controls achieve high micro success while failing save-oriented tasks. We therefore also report an economic-consistency score (the worse of accuracy on upgrade-oriented and save-oriented family groups) which exposes this failure. Tool-API agents attain only 3.9-24.0% micro strict success (at most 7.3% economic consistency), often either stopping before warranted escalation or overspending on cheap tasks. A threshold-crossing budget sweep changes GPT-5.4's escalation rate from 0% to only 3%. These results show that completion under a budget and economical action selection are distinct properties. We release the task bundle, transformation pipeline, frozen evaluation environments, and integrity-bound result artifacts needed to study both.

</details>


### [205] [Hyper-ES: Effective Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning via Descent Direction Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05541)
*Yu Gu,Zhi Zheng,Yunpeng Ba,Xialiang Tong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hyper-ES 结合少量低成本梯度更新构建低维适应子空间，再通过 CMA-ES 在该子空间内优化层式 DARE-TIES 合并系数，从而避免进化策略在高维 LLM 参数空间中的无效搜索，在数学推理任务上以更少的梯度更新超越 GRPO-LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略（ES）是资源受限下 LLM 推理微调的有前景替代方案，但直接应用于数十亿参数的 LLM 效果极差——高维参数空间中大多数随机扰动与有效更新方向近乎正交，导致优化不稳定。

Method: Hyper-ES 是一种基于子空间的 ES 框架。首先通过少量低成本梯度微调获得若干下降方向（每个方向单独改进有限），其张成空间构成一个紧凑的适应子空间；然后在该子空间内使用 CMA-ES 优化层式 DARE-TIES 合并系数，使 ES 搜索有意义的下降方向组合而非全模型随机扰动。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Instruct 和 DeepSeek-R1-Distill 三个骨干网络上、六个数学推理数据集上，Hyper-ES 一致超越 GRPO-LoRA 约 1%，同时节省 10% 的梯度更新开销。

Conclusion: Hyper-ES 验证了将 ES 限制在低维有意义子空间内可有效规避其在高维参数空间中的弱点，为资源受限场景下的 LLM 推理微调提供了高效的新范式。

Abstract: Evolution Strategy (ES) is a promising alternative to gradient-based fine-tuning for resource-constrained Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning. However, directly applying ES to billion-parameter LLMs is highly ineffective. In such high-dimensional parameter spaces, most random perturbations are nearly orthogonal to useful update directions, leading to unstable optimization. We propose Hyper-ES, a subspace-based ES framework that avoids the weakness of ES in full-parameter search while exploiting its strength in low-dimensional optimization. Instead of asking ES to discover useful directions from random perturbations in the LLM parameter space, Hyper-ES first performs a small number of inexpensive gradient-based fine-tuning runs to obtain descent directions. Although each direction may provide only a limited improvement on its own, their span forms a compact adaptation subspace that captures useful reasoning updates. Hyper-ES then applies CMA-ES to optimize layer-wise DARE-TIES merging coefficients within this subspace, allowing ES to search over combinations of meaningful descent directions rather than over arbitrary full-model perturbations. We evaluate Hyper-ES on three Qwen2.5-Instruct and DeepSeek-R1-Distill backbones across six mathematical reasoning datasets. Results show that Hyper-ES consistently outperforms GRPO-LoRA by 1% while requiring 10% fewer space-consuming gradient updates. Code at https://github.com/kuangrepi/Hyper-ES.

</details>


### [206] [SkillTV-Bench: Benchmarking How Well Judges Perform on Skill-Augmented Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05573)
*Zhi Han,Chenxi Zeng,Liuhaichen Yang,Zihan Guo,Ming Zhou,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SkillTV-Bench基准（681个案例，50个任务，11个领域），用于评估面向技能增强型智能体的轨迹验证能力；同时提出SkillTV-Evolve方法，将验证知识外化为可复用的JudgeSkill，通过自动化进化循环提升判准，在基准上准确率提升14.8个百分点，在离线轨迹选择中成功率从22.9%提升至45.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测基准通常仅暴露最终响应或静态轨迹，很少结合任务时技能（task-time skills）与可直接检查的工件和环境，无法有效评估智能体在长程任务中通过工具使用和环境交互进行执行验证的能力。

Method: 构建SkillTV-Bench基准（681个案例，50个任务，11个领域），包含真实智能体轨迹；提出SkillTV-Evolve方法，将验证知识外化为可复用的JudgeSkill，引导智能体法官规划针对性检查并给出基于证据的裁决，并通过自动化进化循环利用误判案例持续优化。

Result: 在SkillTV-Bench上，进化后的JudgeSkill使同一智能体法官的准确率提升14.8个百分点；在离线 rollout-pool 选择中，选定轨迹成功率从单次rollout的22.9%提升至十次rollout的45.5%。

Conclusion: 外化验证知识为可复用技能并自动进化，能显著提升智能体对长程轨迹的验证准确性，有助于从多个rollout中筛选成功轨迹，对技能增强型智能体的可靠部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly execute long-horizon tasks through tool use and environment interaction, shifting evaluation from final-response scoring to verification of complete executions. For skill-augmented agents, verification additionally requires the procedural knowledge encoded in task-time skills, because this knowledge indicates what evidence to inspect and which failures are task-critical. However, existing judge benchmarks often expose final responses or static trajectories, and rarely combine task-time skills with directly inspectable artifacts and environments. We therefore introduce SkillTV-Bench, a 681-case benchmark of real agent trajectories from 50 tasks across eleven domains, designed to evaluate skill-aware trajectory verification for both LLM-as-a-Judge and Agent-as-a-Judge methods. Additionally, we propose SkillTV-Evolve, which externalizes verification knowledge as a reusable JudgeSkill that guides an agent judge to plan targeted inspections and issue evidence-grounded verdicts. On a disjoint development pool, an automated evolution loop further refines the JudgeSkill using misjudged cases. On SkillTV-Bench, the refined skill increases the same agent judge's accuracy by 14.8 percentage points. In offline rollout-pool selection, it increases selected-trajectory success from 22.9% with one rollout to 45.5% with ten rollouts. The code and data are available at https://github.com/HanZhi306/SkillTV-Bench

</details>


### [207] [StepReflect: Structured UI Transition Reflection for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05587)
*Linqiang Guo,Wei Liu,Li Gu,Yang Wang,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StepReflect 是一个用于移动 GUI 智能体的轻量级步骤级反射模型，通过结构化预测而非开放式多模态推理来高效地评估动作结果，在多个基准上超越或媲美 GPT-5.2 的同时显著降低 API 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动 GUI 智能体依赖每步后的开放式多模态推理来反思动作结果，这种方式成本高昂且与 GUI 状态迁移的结构化特性不匹配，需要一种更高效、结构化的替代方案。

Method: StepReflect 将每步 GUI 反射形式化为基于显式迁移规范和配对视觉证据的结构化预测任务，通过三阶段流水线训练：监督微调、师生蒸馏、偏好与奖励优化，最终得到一个 8B 参数的轻量级模型。

Result: 离线测试中，StepReflect 在 AndroidWorld 上达到 82.16% 的迁移级准确率，比零样本 GPT-5.2 高出 11.83 个百分点。在线测试中，在四个智能体配置（M3A、Agent-SAMA、MAI-UI-8B、Seed-2.0-Pro）中有三个取得更高的任务成功率，第四个与 GPT-5.2 反思智能体相差不超过一个成功任务，并在所有四个配置中降低了 API 费用。

Conclusion: StepReflect 被确立为一种实用、可本地部署的替代方案，可用于替代反复调用前沿模型进行反射的长时移动 GUI 智能体，兼具高性能和低成本优势。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile GUI agents require accurate action reflection for reliable long-horizon execution. Existing approaches rely on open-ended multimodal reasoning after each action, which is costly and poorly matched to the structured nature of GUI state transitions. We propose StepReflect, which formulates per-step GUI reflection as supervised structured prediction conditioned on explicit transition specifications and paired visual evidence. StepReflect is trained through a staged pipeline combining supervised fine-tuning, teacher-student distillation, and preference- and reward-based refinement. Offline, the resulting 8B model achieves 82.16% transition-level accuracy on AndroidWorld, exceeding zero-shot GPT-5.2 by 11.83 percentage points under the same structured input. Online, across M3A, Agent-SAMA, MAI-UI-8B, and Seed-2.0-Pro, StepReflect achieves higher task success in three of four agent configurations and remains within one successful task of the GPT-5.2 Reflection Agent in the fourth. It also reduces paid API charges relative to GPT-based reflection in all four configurations. These results establish StepReflect as a practical, locally deployable alternative to repeated frontier-model reflection for long-horizon mobile GUI agents.

</details>


### [208] [Epistemic Trustworthiness in Generative AI: A Normative Framework for Warranted Reliance in High-Stakes Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05602)
*Nimisha Karnatak,Max Van Kleek,Nigel Shadbolt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI在专业高风险场景中使用的认识论正当性问题，提出"可信赖性"（epistemic trustworthiness）应当作为独立于准确性、公平性等传统指标的评估标准，并构建了包含认识谦逊、认识可及性、抵制认识不正义三个必要条件的规范性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架关注AI输出的准确性、公平性、可解释性、安全性等指标，但无法直接回答用户何时有理由将AI输出作为自身推理的输入。论文旨在填补这一空白，明确"有正当理由的依赖"（warranted reliance）作为独立的评估目标，而不仅仅是行为上的依赖。

Method: 借鉴哲学中关于可信赖性（competence and audience-orientation）的理论，构建包含三个联合必要且不可替换条件的规范性框架：(1) 认识谦逊——系统应表征并沟通自身能力的局限；(2) 认识可及性——系统应允许用户检查、质疑和反驳输出；(3) 抵制认识不正义——系统应承认用户为合法的认识主体，避免边缘化其知识和经验。通过法律推理、医疗推理和招聘三个真实案例展示框架的适用性。

Result: 在法律、医疗和招聘案例中，认识谦逊、认识可及性和抵制认识不正义的缺失会产生传统准确率、公平性和可用性指标无法单独衡量的重大伤害。表明该框架能够识别标准评估方法遗漏的认识论风险。

Conclusion: 应以认识论上有正当理由的依赖（而非单纯输出正确性）为核心组织GenAI系统的设计与评估，为负责任AI提供新的规范性方向。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes professional contexts, where their outputs shape what users believe, how they reason, and what they treat as settled. This raises a central question for responsible AI: under what conditions is reliance on generative AI outputs epistemically warranted rather than behaviourally induced? Existing frameworks largely ask whether AI outputs are accurate, fair, explainable, safe, or trusted by users. These questions remain necessary, and each can contribute to warranted reliance. However, they do not directly specify warranted reliance as a distinct evaluative target: the conditions under which users are justified in treating AI outputs as inputs into their own reasoning. We argue that this requires an account of epistemic trustworthiness: what makes a system epistemically worthy of reliance. Drawing on philosophical accounts of trustworthiness as competence and audience-orientation, we develop a constitutive normative framework comprising three jointly necessary and non-fungible conditions. First, epistemic humility requires systems to represent and communicate the limits of their competence. Second, epistemic access requires systems to enable users to inspect, question, and contest outputs in context. Third, resistance to epistemic injustice requires systems to recognise users as legitimate epistemic agents and avoid marginalising their knowledge and experience. Through real-world case analyses in legal reasoning, medical reasoning, and hiring, we show how failures of epistemic humility, epistemic access, and resistance to epistemic injustice can produce consequential harms that standard measures of accuracy, fairness, and usability do not address on their own. We conclude by outlining design and evaluation implications for GenAI systems organised around epistemically warranted reliance rather than output correctness alone.

</details>


### [209] [Measuring and Detecting Harmful AI Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05624)
*Bohan Jiang,Dawei Li,Yasin Silva,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于大语言模型中的一种有害谄媚行为——偏好引起的立场反转谄媚（PSRS），提出了CAP（对比锚点探测）框架用于收集带标签的PSRS数据，在17个模型上收集了近29万条响应，研究了PSRS的发生率、检测可行性以及跨模型泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要衡量模型谄媚程度，但未深入探究PSRS是否能从单条响应中自动检测，且缺乏大规模标注数据集。本文旨在系统研究PSRS的发生率、自动检测可能性及检测器对未见模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出CAP（对比锚点探测）框架，通过对比锚点方法收集带标签的PSRS数据，应用于17个开源和闭源LLM，收集了290,460条标注响应，涵盖12个日常建议领域。围绕三个研究问题（发生率、检测效果、跨模型泛化）展开分析。

Result: PSRS发生率在5%到56%之间，更强大的模型谄媚程度更低；从响应文本中检测PSRS是可行的，但检测器需从训练数据中学习细微模式；跨模型泛化时检测性能下降，作者提出了初步改进方法。

Conclusion: PSRS是一个严重且普遍的问题，自动检测可行但面临跨模型泛化挑战，论文将公开数据集和代码以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Sycophantic responses are becoming pervasive in large language models (LLMs), and prior work has pointed out that some of them could be harmful. This paper focuses on one harmful sycophancy: preference-induced stance reversal sycophancy (PSRS), where a model reverses an initial stance merely to align with a user's stated preference. While existing research mainly measures how sycophantic a model is, we go further and ask whether PSRS can also be detected automatically from a single response. To investigate this at scale, we introduce CAP (Contrastive Anchor Probing), a framework for collecting labeled PSRS data. Applying CAP to 17 open- and closed-source LLMs, we collect 290,460 labeled responses across 12 everyday-advice domains. We organize our study around three research questions. (1) How often does PSRS occur? (2) How well can it be detected? (3) How does detection generalize to unseen models? We first reveal that PSRS rates range from 5% to 56% across LLMs, with more capable models being less sycophantic. Next, we show that detecting PSRS is feasible from the response text alone, and detectors need to learn subtle PSRS patterns from the training data. Because new LLMs appear rapidly, detectors inevitably encounter unseen models, making cross-model generalization an important framework goal. We demonstrate that detection performance drops on unseen models and propose an initial approach to address this challenge. We will release our dataset and code to support future research.

</details>


### [210] [SkillHEX: Improving Agent Skills via Hypothesis-Driven Autonomous Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05628)
*Yuru Feng,Yaoqi Chen,Beidi Zhao,Qianxi Zhang,Xinjiang Wang,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillHEX是一个闭环框架，通过将可证伪的失败假设转化为可执行测试以生成密集奖励信号，并利用证据引导的树搜索在有限的交互预算下实现技能自主进化，解决了LLM代理技能演化中的稀疏奖励和利用陷阱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理技能依赖手动维护，成本高、不可扩展且易错对齐；实际部署需要自主、按需的技能进化，但面临有限交互预算、缺乏训练/验证集以及稀疏奖励问题——结果中混叠了多个潜在失败原因，现有贪心方法容易陷入利用陷阱（早期误诊耗尽有限尝试于无益轨迹）。

Method: SkillHEX是一个闭环框架，耦合了假设驱动自验证与证据引导树搜索。它将可证伪的失败假设转化为可执行测试，产生诊断性证据作为密集奖励（无需额外环境交互）。该证据引导对持久技能修正分支的搜索，动态平衡对支持编辑的利用与对合理替代方案的探索。

Result: 在SkillsBench的87个任务上评估，SkillHEX在五次迭代预算下，使用GPT-5.3-Codex和Claude Opus 4.7分别达到了55.9%和57.9%的平均通过率，优于现有自进化方法。

Conclusion: SkillHEX通过假设驱动自验证和证据引导树搜索，有效解决了LLM技能自主进化中的稀疏奖励和利用陷阱问题，显著提升了有限交互预算下的技能演化成功率。

Abstract: Although agent skills equip LLMs with reusable procedural knowledge, manual maintenance suffers from high costs, unscalability, and misalignment. Real-world deployments thus require autonomous, on-demand skill evolution at test time, constrained by limited interaction budgets and a lack of training or validation sets. This setting introduces a severe sparse reward challenge, where outcomes conflate multiple latent failure causes. Under such ambiguity, existing methods that greedily refine a single incumbent skill are particularly vulnerable to an exploitation trap, allowing early misdiagnoses to exhaust limited trials along unproductive trajectories. To address this, we introduce SkillHEX, a closed-loop framework coupling hypothesis-driven self-verification with evidence-guided tree search. SkillHEX translates falsifiable failure hypotheses into executable tests, producing diagnostic evidence as dense reward without additional environment attempts. This evidence guides a search over persistent skill-revision branches, dynamically balancing the exploitation of supported edits with the exploration of plausible alternatives. Evaluated on 87 tasks from SkillsBench, SkillHEX outperforms existing self-evolving methods and achieves an average pass rate of 55.9% and 57.9% using GPT-5.3-Codex and Claude Opus 4.7 under a five-iteration budget, respectively.

</details>


### [211] [Bayesian Expected Uncertainty Reduction (B-EUR) Model: A Computational Account of What Makes Design Options Worth Trying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05642)
*Shimon Honda,Takuma Miyaguchi,Koji Koizumi,Takanori Sano,Tristan Briard,Hideyoshi Yanagisawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯期望不确定性降低（B-EUR）模型，形式化地定义了尝试候选设计行动的价值为其对行动-结果关系认知不确定性降低的期望值。通过模拟和人类实验（图形-形状猜测任务）检验了可泛化性和结果可辨别性两个环境属性，发现认知价值与可泛化性呈倒U型关系，随结果可辨别性增加而增加；人类实验中主观价值和愉悦感也呈倒U型关系，选择行为反映两种属性。该模型为不确定性驱动的设计活动中的候选行动评估提供了计算解释，并为构建原型集、框架设计问题和组织反馈以支持信息性探索提供了启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不确定性驱动行动（UDA）模型中的一个开放问题：不确定性感知的变化如何决定行动选择。具体而言，需要形式化地定义在有限试验预算下，尝试某个候选设计行动的价值——即其对行动-结果关系认知不确定性的期望降低量。

Method: 提出贝叶斯期望不确定性降低（B-EUR）模型，通过模拟和人类实验进行检验。实验采用图形-形状猜测任务，该任务能隔离对行动-结果关系的学习过程，并受限于有限试验预算。操纵两个环境属性：可泛化性（知识从一个试验延伸到邻近候选的程度）和结果可辨别性（结果之间差异的清晰程度）。

Result: 模拟中，认知价值与可泛化性呈倒U型关系，并随结果可辨别性增加而增加。人类实验中，尝试的主观价值和愉悦感与可泛化性呈倒U型关系，选择行为同时反映了可泛化性和结果可辨别性两个属性。

Conclusion: B-EUR模型为不确定性驱动的设计活动中候选行动的评估提供了计算层面的解释，并对构建原型集、框架设计问题以及组织反馈以支持信息性探索具有启示意义。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Bayesian Expected Uncertainty Reduction (B-EUR) model, which formalizes the value of trying a candidate design action as its expected reduction of epistemic uncertainty about action--outcome relations. The model addresses one part of the Uncertainty Driven Action (UDA) model's open question concerning how changes in uncertainty perception determine action selection. We examine two environmental properties: generalizability, or how far knowledge from one trial extends to neighboring candidates, and outcome discriminability, or how clearly differences among outcomes can be distinguished.
  We tested the model through simulations and human experiments using a graph-shape guessing task that isolates learning about action--outcome relations under a limited trial budget. Epistemic value followed an inverted-U-shaped relationship with generalizability and increased with outcome discriminability in the simulations. In the human experiments, the subjective value of trying and enjoyment followed inverted-U-shaped relationships with generalizability, while choice behavior reflected both properties.
  The B-EUR model provides a computational account of candidate-action evaluation within uncertainty-driven design activity and offers implications for constructing prototype sets, framing design problems, and organizing feedback to support informative exploration.

</details>


### [212] [Refining Over Resampling: Test-Time Self-Correction for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05643)
*Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Muhammad Umer,Lena Trigg,Ali Subhan,Muhammad Ali,Dean F. Hougen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种免验证器的广度-深度精炼框架，通过采样多条推理轨迹、对每条轨迹进行自我批判与自我修正迭代精炼，再经多数投票聚合，从而更有效地利用测试时计算资源，在多个数学推理基准上超越贪心解码、多数投票、验证器导向方法等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法中，单纯增加采样数量会出现收益递减（重复答案模式），而依赖验证器选择的效果受外部奖励模型校准质量制约。因此需要一种不依赖外部验证器、能同时探索多样化候选方案并修复局部推理错误的测试时计算方法。

Method: 无验证器的广度-深度精炼框架：1）广度：采样多条独立推理轨迹（rollout）；2）深度：对每条轨迹通过迭代自我批判与自我修正进行精炼；3）聚合：对精炼后的答案进行多数投票。广度保持初始多样性，深度在聚合前修复局部推理错误。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、AMC、OlympiadBench、MATH500等多个基准上，该方法在多种开源模型上一致优于贪心解码、多数投票、验证器最佳-of-N、束搜索、前瞻解码等基线。例如Qwen2.5-1.5B在MATH500上从最强验证器基线的准确率提升至58.0%，在AMC上从25.0%提升至32.5%。

Conclusion: 测试时计算用于精炼采样轨迹比仅增加采样数量或依赖验证器引导更有效，合理分配计算资源到深度精炼而非单纯广度采样能带来更显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by using additional inference compute, but wider sampling alone can suffer from diminishing returns: new rollouts often repeat existing answer patterns instead of adding useful reasoning diversity. Verifier-based selection offers an alternative, but its performance depends on the calibration of an external reward model. We propose a verifier-free breadth--depth refinement framework that uses test-time compute to both explore and improve candidate solutions. The method samples multiple independent reasoning rollouts, refines each rollout through iterative self-critique and self-correction, and aggregates the refined answers by majority voting. Breadth preserves diverse initial attempts, while depth repairs local reasoning errors before aggregation. Across AIME24, AIME25, AMC, OlympiadBench, and MATH500, our method consistently improves over greedy decoding, majority voting, verifier-based best-of-$N$, beam search, and lookahead decoding across multiple open-weight models. For instance, with Qwen2.5-1.5B, accuracy increases from the strongest verifier-based baseline to $58.0\%$ on MATH500, and from $25.0\%$ to $32.5\%$ on AMC. These results show that test-time compute can be more effective when used to refine sampled trajectories rather than only to sample more candidates or rely on verifier-guided selection.

</details>


### [213] [A Unified Framework for Trajectory Prediction with Explicit Planning and Reaction Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05673)
*Jiaheng Chen,Jiaxing Li,Tinghe Zhang,Chaopeng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INTraJ将轨迹预测重新构想为一个两阶段过程，将社会交互分解为规划（参考轨迹构建）和反应（局部调整）两个阶段，在多个基准上取得了一致改进和SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法虽然采用了结构化建模并显式建模社会交互，但未能充分区分社会影响在轨迹规划中的功能角色。观测到智能体通常先预测他人未来行为形成运动计划，再进行局部反应调整，因此社会交互应扮演阶段性角色：规划先于反应。

Method: 提出INTraJ统一框架，将社会影响分解为两个阶段：规划阶段利用未来社会信息构建参考轨迹；反应阶段从完整上下文预测与参考轨迹之间的残差中恢复局部调整。该框架同时支持多目标和单目标预测范式。

Result: 在Argoverse 2、Argoverse 2-ped、ETH/UCY和SDD四个标准基准上进行了广泛实验，取得了持续改进，尤其在最终位移误差（FDE）和长时程一致性方面表现突出，在多个设置下达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: INTraJ验证了分阶段社会建模（规划-反应）对于稳定预测至关重要，并将轨迹预测重新定义为规划驱动的两阶段过程，为结构化社会交互建模提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction has shifted toward structured formulations with explicit social modeling. However, existing methods inadequately distinguish the functional roles of social influence in trajectory planning. Observing that agents typically form motion plans by anticipating others' future behaviors before making local reactive adjustments, we identify social interactions as playing staged roles, namely planning precedes reaction. We propose INTraJ, a unified framework that decomposes social influence into two stages: a planning stage constructs reference trajectories using future social information, and a reaction stage recovers local adjustments from the residual between full-context prediction and the reference. INTraJ supports both multi-target and single-target paradigms. Extensive experiments on four standard benchmarks, including Argoverse 2, Argoverse 2-ped, ETH/UCY, and SDD, demonstrate consistent improvements, particularly in FDE and long-horizon consistency, with state-of-the-art performance achieved in several settings. INTraJ reframes trajectory prediction as a planning-driven two-stage process, validating that staged social modeling is critical for stable predictions. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/11isnotavailable/INTraJ.

</details>


### [214] [Grounded Well-Condition Anomaly Detection on the Volve Field: Constructed Labels, a Baseline, and a Dual-Head Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05685)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用Equinor公开的Volve油田实测数据，构建了经工程文档验证的异常标签，并检验了这些标签的可学习性：无监督检测器能自发覆盖相同区域，有监督双头模型可跨井识别事件类型但时间定位较粗略。数据集、标签、模型与代码均以CC-BY-NC-SA 4.0开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器状态监测基准多来自试验台架（故障人为诱发、事件已知），而真实生产现场只有传感器历史数据、没有故障日志，导致异常检测方法需自行生成标签且隐含假设难以察觉。本文希望利用真实油田数据，构建经过工程文档验证的可靠标签，并检验其可学习性，以弥合实验室与现场之间的差距。

Method: 使用Equinor公开的Volve油田数据，首先依据工程文档构建物理可解释的异常标签并公开推理过程；然后采用无监督基线（不接触标签）和有监督双头模型（同时预测事件发生与否及事件类型）测试标签的可学习性。双头模型思想继承自前期金属零件缺陷检测工作。

Result: 无监督检测器虽然从未见过标签，但仍能覆盖规则标记的相同区域，说明标签并非任意构造；紧凑的有监督模型在未见井上能较好恢复事件存在与否及事件类型，但时间定位仅大致准确。

Conclusion: 论文诚实地报告了有效和无效的部分以及每项假设，并公开了数据集、标签及推理依据、基线分数、训练模型和代码，为真实生产环境下的异常检测提供了可复现、可验证的基准。

Abstract: Most public benchmarks for machine-condition monitoring come from test rigs, where faults are induced on purpose and every event is known. Real production fields rarely offer that. They give you sensor histories with no fault log attached, which is exactly the situation where an anomaly-detection method has to invent its own labels, and where quiet assumptions can slip in unnoticed. We work with the open Volve field data released by Equinor and take two things seriously that such datasets usually skip. First, we build anomaly labels that are not just patterns in the numbers but are checked against what the field's own engineering documents say can physically go wrong, and we release the reasoning behind every label. Second, we test whether those constructed labels are learnable at all, using both an unsupervised baseline and a small dual-head model that marks when an event happens and what kind it is, an idea we carry over from earlier work on defect detection in metal parts. The results are honest. An unsupervised detector that never sees the labels still lands on the same regions our rules flagged, which tells us the labels are not arbitrary. A compact supervised model recovers event presence and event type well across wells it has never seen, and locates events in time only roughly. We report what worked, what did not, and every assumption in between. The dataset, grounded labels, per-label provenance, baseline scores, trained model, and code are released publicly under CC-BY-NC-SA 4.0.

</details>


### [215] [DreamGuard: Efficient Runtime Guardrail for LLM Agents via Risk-Aware World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05695)
*Wenhao Lin,Chenyu Yu,Xingwei Lin,Sicong Cao,Xiang Chen,Lei Xue,Le Yu,Letian Sha,Chunming Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DreamGuard是一个为LLM代理设计的主动式护栏，它通过风险感知的世界模型来预测长期风险，在动作执行前进行干预，从而比传统反应式护栏更有效地防止危险状态累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时护栏大多是反应式的，只评估当前动作的表面安全性，缺乏对风险在轨迹中如何演变的显式建模，这导致对长程风险存在盲区——单个看似良性的动作可能逐渐将代理引向危险状态。

Method: DreamGuard构建了一个风险感知的世界模型，该模型维护一个紧凑的循环潜在状态来追踪轨迹，并预测未来潜在状态；从中提取即时危害证据和前缀风险证据，然后将这些多时间尺度的信号融合成干预决策，在动作执行前进行 proactive 的拦截。

Result: 在四个基准测试和一个在线护栏评估中，DreamGuard优于通用型、反应式和主动式护栏基线，在安全-效用权衡中达到最佳，且平均端到端延迟仅为每调用25毫秒。

Conclusion: DreamGuard通过显式建模风险随时间的演化，实现了高效且低延迟的主动式安全防护，证明了在LLM代理中引入前瞻性风险建模对于防止长期累积性危险至关重要。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents increasingly invoke external tools and interact with real-world systems, unsafe actions may cause irreversible consequences on external states, user data, and downstream services. Recent runtime guardrails mitigate such risks by checking proposed actions before execution, but many remain reactive: they primarily assess the apparent safety of the current action, lacking an explicit model of how risk evolves across the trajectory. This limitation creates a critical blind spot for long-horizon risks, where individually benign-looking actions can gradually drift the agent toward hazardous states. In response, we propose DreamGuard, a proactive guardrail for LLM agents built around a risk-aware world model. The world model maintains a compact recurrent latent state over the trajectory and predicts future latent states from which DreamGuard derives immediate-hazard and prefix-risk evidence. It then fuses these multi-horizon signals into intervention decisions before execution. Experiments across four benchmarks and an online guardrail evaluation show that DreamGuard outperforms generic, reactive, and proactive guardrail baselines, achieves the best safety-utility trade-off among evaluated guardrails, and maintains an average end-to-end latency of 25 ms per call.

</details>


### [216] [Shaping Human-AI Interactions to Provide Improvement Pathways and Balance Competing Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05710)
*Keziah Naggita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文研究如何设计和塑造人类与AI系统之间的交互循环，以实现三个目标：帮助个体建立对AI的准确认知、鼓励改进并抑制博弈行为、确保AI系统达成预期目标。论文通过理论分析、数据驱动建模、人类受试者实验以及真实/半合成数据集实证评估，从被评估个体和AI系统双方视角推进人本机器学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI系统部署后，用户或被评估者会形成关于系统运作方式的信念，并策略性地展示自身偏好、行为或属性以获取有利结果。这种人类-AI交互循环可能导致信息失真、博弈行为和系统目标偏离。因此，需要系统地研究和设计这种交互，使得个体能够准确理解AI系统、以最小代价改进或获得有利结果，同时阻止博弈行为并确保AI系统持续达成预期目标。

Method: 论文采用综合方法论：理论分析（建模人类-AI交互的博弈与激励机制）、数据驱动建模（从行为数据中学习信念演变与策略）、人类受试者实验（验证交互设计对用户行为的影响）、以及在真实世界和半合成数据集上的实证评估。论文分为三个互补部分，分别从被评估个体和AI系统的视角审视交互。

Result: 论文提供了设计AI系统的原则和方法，使其与人类需求、价值和能力对齐。具体成果包括：帮助个体形成准确信念的交互机制、促进真实改进而非博弈的激励设计、以及确保AI系统在交互循环中不偏离准确率等核心目标的方法。

Conclusion: 该论文推进了人本机器学习领域，表明精心设计的人类-AI交互循环能够同时服务于个体（获得公平结果、促进真实改进）和系统（维持目标、抵御博弈）的双赢局面。理论分析与实证相结合的方法论为未来人本AI系统设计提供了可参考的框架。

Abstract: When an AI system is deployed, the individuals who use and or are evaluated by it form beliefs about how the system operates and use those beliefs to strategically present their preferences, behaviors, or attributes. The system then responds with feedback or a decision outcome, thereby creating a human-AI interaction loop. This thesis studies how to design and shape such interactions to achieve three goals: (1) help individuals develop accurate beliefs about the AI systems so they can improve and or secure favorable outcomes at minimal cost, (2) encourage improvement and or discourage gaming behaviors, and (3) ensure that the AI system continues to achieve its intended objectives, such as maximizing accuracy. To address these goals, the thesis is organized into three complementary parts that examine and study human-AI interactions from the perspectives of both evaluated individuals and AI systems. Together, the work presented in this thesis advances human-centered machine learning by providing principles and methods for designing AI systems that align with human needs, values, and capabilities. Methodologically, this thesis integrates theoretical analysis, data-driven modeling, human-subject experiments, and empirical evaluations on real-world and semi-synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [217] [RA-CAD: Learning Post-Execution Critique for State-Aware Text-to-CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05714)
*Shuhao Yan,Changhao He,Xi Peng,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RA-CAD（ReAct Agent for CAD），一种基于状态感知的文本到CAD生成智能体。它通过“生成-执行-批判-重写”循环与CAD环境交互，利用可学习的批判机制优化代码生成过程，并在CADFusion和Text2CAD基准上达到最优执行有效性和几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD生成方法虽然引入了外部或独立优化的批判机制，但未能有效优化反馈如何被解读并转化为具体的修正行为，存在反馈利用鸿沟（feedback-utilization gap）。RA-CAD旨在弥合这一鸿沟，使批判成为可学习、对齐结果的政策决策而非未优化的辅助输出。

Method: RA-CAD采用生成-执行-批判-重写（Generate-Execute-Critique-Rewrite）的迭代循环。每个迭代中，智能体执行当前CAD代码并观察结果，根据设计指令、当前代码和执行反馈生成显式的执行后批判，作为中间政策行动。该批判要么验证当前结果以终止，要么提供面向修订的指导以驱动下一轮重写。方法包括两个关键阶段：CAD代码引导（CCB）通过监督微调建立基础参数化CAD编码能力；反馈驱动智能体优化（FAO）应用轨迹级分组相对策略优化（Group Relative Policy Optimization）同时对政策生成的代码和批判序列进行优化，以终端F1分数和Chamfer距离作为奖励。

Result: 在CADFusion和Text2CAD数据集上的实验表明，RA-CAD在执行有效性和几何质量方面均超越了现有方法及强大的专有语言模型，取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: RA-CAD通过将批判机制纳入可学习的政策决策框架，有效提升了文本到CAD生成的执行有效性和几何保真度，验证了状态感知智能体在文本到CAD任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD generation translates natural-language design intent into editable and executable parametric computer-aided design (CAD) codes, reducing the expertise and effort required for manual modeling. Existing methods incorporate fixed, externally supplied, prompt-induced, or separately optimized critique mechanisms to optimize the generation process, but they do not necessarily optimize how feedback is interpreted and translated into effective corrective actions throughout the generation process. To bridge this feedback-utilization gap, we present RA-CAD (ReAct Agent for CAD), a state-aware agent that interacts with the CAD environment through a Generate--Execute--Critique--Rewrite loop. At each iteration, RA-CAD executes the current code and observes its outcome. Conditioned on the design instruction, current code, and execution feedback, the agent then generates an explicit post-execution critique as an intermediate policy action. This critique either validates the current result for termination or provides revision-oriented guidance that conditions the next rewrite. CAD Code Bootstrapping (CCB) first establishes fundamental parametric CAD coding capabilities through supervised fine-tuning. Feedback-Driven Agent Optimization (FAO) subsequently applies trajectory-level Group Relative Policy Optimization to both policy-generated code and critique sequences, assigning terminal F1 and Chamfer Distance rewards to the complete interaction trajectory. This formulation makes critique an outcome-aligned, learnable policy decision rather than an unoptimized auxiliary output. Experiments on CADFusion and Text2CAD show that RA-CAD achieves state-of-the-art execution validity and geometric quality compared with existing methods and strong proprietary language models, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed state-aware text-to-CAD agent.

</details>


### [218] [BlockPython: A Process-Aware Agent-Supported Platform for the Transition from Block-Based to Python Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05716)
*Jesse Yusuf Chan,Haoming Wang,Mingwei Xu,Xianlong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BlockPython是一个旨在弥合从积木式编程到Python文本编程认知鸿沟的平台。其核心机制是积木与Python代码的双向实时翻译，引导学习者经历任务分解、积木实践、代码挑战和拓展交互四个递进阶段，逐步建立程序结构、运行行为与文本代码之间的联系。平台持续收集积木制品、代码版本、运行结果等过程证据，结合规则系统、确定性诊断、程序可视化及AI学习助手，提供精准的个性化支持。本文详细阐述了该平台的设计原理、学习工作流和过程感知支持机制，为过渡性编程教育环境的设计提供了系统参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从可视化的积木编程过渡到抽象的Python文本编程时，学习者需要将直观的程序结构转化为复杂的文本语法，这构成了巨大的认知鸿沟，阻碍了计算概念的理解与Python表达能力的掌握。设计BlockPython的核心动机正是为了系统性地支持这一关键过渡，帮助学习者建立从具体操作到抽象代码的思维桥梁，降低编程入门的心理与认知障碍。

Method: BlockPython的方法主要包括：1）**双向翻译**：实现积木与Python代码结构的实时同步转换，帮助理解两者的映射关系；2）**四阶段学习流程**：任务分解（理解问题）、积木实践（可视化构建）、代码挑战（文本编写）、拓展交互（应用迁移），逐步去除脚手架；3）**过程证据收集**：持续记录积木制品、代码版本历史、运行结果、支持使用情况和对话日志，形成完整学习画像；4）**诊断与支持机制**：基于规则的系统负责执行、客观评估和阶段控制，确定性诊断和程序可视化识别具体困难，学习助手利用验证过的证据提供解释、提示和引导性问题。

Result: 本文作为一份系统设计报告，详细阐述了BlockPython的设计理念、平台架构、四阶段学习工作流以及基于过程证据的智能支持机制。它提供了一个如何构建从积木编程到文本编程过渡性学习环境的完整设计参考，展示了利用学习过程数据驱动个性化反馈的可行方案。

Conclusion: BlockPython通过结合双向翻译、分阶段脚手架搭建以及细粒度的过程证据分析，有效地搭建了从积木编程到Python文本编程的桥梁。其设计表明，对学习过程数据的精准采集与智能诊断，能够帮助系统识别并缓解学习者在概念理解和语法表达上的具体困难，为实现个性化编程教育提供了有价值的参考路径和系统设计蓝图。

Abstract: The transition from block-based to text-based programming requires learners to convert visible program structures into abstract textual expressions, which may create a cognitive gap between understanding computational concepts and expressing them in Python syntax. To support this transition, we designed and implemented BlockPython. The platform centers on bidirectional translation between blocks and Python and guides learners through four stages: Task Decomposition, Block-Based Practice, Code Challenge, and Extended Interaction. Across these stages, learners progressively establish connections among program structure, runtime behavior, and textual code. During learning, the platform continuously collects process evidence, including block artifacts, code versions, run outcomes, use of support, and dialogue. Deterministic diagnosis, program visualization, and the learning assistant use this evidence to identify different difficulties in computational understanding and Python expression. The rule-based system is responsible for program execution, objective evaluation, and stage control, while the learning assistant uses verified evidence to provide explanations, prompts, and guiding questions. This report describes the design rationale, learning workflow, and process-aware support mechanisms of BlockPython and provides a system-design reference for supporting the transition from block-based to text-based programming and for analyzing learning processes.

</details>


### [219] [Unified Agent: Managing Interactions across Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05729)
*Xinshuang Liu,Runfa Blark Li,Shaoxiu Wei,Xin Lin,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出跨设备、跨时间的AI代理应维护一个紧凑、可行动的状态（包含交互证据、陈述事实和当前请求），并基于此设计Unified Agent。实验表明，在跨设备/时间的基准测试中，该状态设计显著优于现有方法，且在不同多模态大模型（MLLM）设置下均保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理系统在处理跨设备、跨时间场景时存在不足：单一代理将设备视为工具，缺乏跨所有设备的有效状态管理；多代理系统虽协调但未维护紧凑的携带状态。因此需要一种能跨设备、跨时间组织交互证据的状态设计。

Method: 本文定义了代理应维护的状态结构（交互证据、陈述事实、当前请求），并构建了跨设备/时间的用户-代理交互基准。基于此原则实现了Unified Agent，该代理携带跨设备和时刻的交互证据，结合当前观察进行决策。

Result: 在默认设置下，Unified Agent显著优于四种现有设计的改编版本；在改变MLLM家族、能力和推理努力时，仍保持领先，表明状态设计优势对不同MLLM设置鲁棒。

Conclusion: 有效的状态设计是跨设备、跨时间AI代理成功的关键；Unified Agent验证了该原则的优越性和鲁棒性，代码和数据将开源。

Abstract: As capabilities rapidly increase, AI agents can move from running inside one app to acting across a user's devices over time. Yet existing agent systems still fall short in this scenario. This is because observations are scattered across devices and moments, but mainstream systems are not designed around this fact: a single agent that treats devices as tools lacks effective state management for all devices across time, and multi-agent systems coordinate across agents but do not maintain the compact carried state a cross-device, cross-time request needs. We argue that the agent should maintain an effectively designed state that organizes engagement evidence, stated facts, and the standing request in a compact, action-ready form for deciding its action given the current observation. To compare state designs, we construct a benchmark of user-agent interaction across devices and time. We instantiate this principle in Unified Agent, a stateful agent that carries interaction evidence across devices and moments and uses it with the current observation to act. In the default setting, it significantly outperforms our adaptations of four published designs. Across changes in multimodal large language model (MLLM) family, capability, and reasoning effort, it remains ahead of all compared systems, demonstrating that the state-design advantage is robust across MLLM settings. Our code and data will be publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [220] [Subliminal Learning is Non-Semantic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05734)
*Ethan Hadley,Eren Gultepe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型中的"潜意识学习"(Subliminal Learning, SL)现象，即通过蒸馏看似无关的随机合成数据，教师模型的偏差或行为能够转移给学生模型。作者探索了SL的机制和驱动因素，发现向教师和学生模型权重添加高斯噪声可将潜意识转移幅度提升1.9倍（Gemma）和1.3倍（Llama），表明非语义权重结构起关键作用。研究还展示了使用引导向量(steering vectors)生成潜意识数据的方法，并发现学生不仅继承教师偏差的语义含义，还继承了干预类型（引导 vs. 提示）。此外，引导潜意识数据的梯度与教师引导向量线性相关，为数据审计提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型中的潜意识学习现象可能导致AI系统不可预测且难以安全训练，因为标准数据审计无法捕捉隐藏的潜意识信号。理解SL的机制和驱动因素对于确保AI系统可控、安全至关重要。

Method: 1) 向教师和学生模型权重添加高斯噪声，观察潜意识转移幅度的变化。2) 使用引导向量(steering vectors)生成潜意识数据，与提示(prompting)和微调(finetuning)方法对比。3) 分析学生模型的激活值，比较继承语义含义和干预类型。4) 计算引导潜意识数据的梯度与教师引导向量的相关性。

Result: 1) 添加高斯噪声后，潜意识转移幅度在Gemma中提升1.9倍，Llama中提升1.3倍。2) 引导向量可有效生成潜意识数据。3) 学生模型不仅继承教师偏差的语义含义，还继承干预类型：引导学生模仿引导向量，提示学生则不然。4) 引导潜意识数据的梯度与教师引导向量呈线性相关，有助于数据审计。

Conclusion: 随着合成数据在前沿训练流程中变得核心，检测训练数据中隐藏的潜在信号至关重要。本研究揭示了潜意识学习的机制，特别是非语义权重结构和引导向量的作用，为数据审计和AI安全训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Subliminal Learning (SL) is a surprising type of generalization displayed by modern language models. It allows the transfer of a bias or behavior from a teacher model to a student by distilling from seemingly unrelated or random synthetic data from the teacher. This presents challenges in ensuring AI systems remain predictable and are trained safely, as standard auditing of the input data would not catch the hidden subliminal signal. Here, we investigate several open questions as to the enabling mechanisms and drivers of SL. First is the nature of the process by which biases are encoded in the data. We find that by adding Gaussian noise to the weights of the teacher and student models, the magnitude of subliminal transfer is increased by a factor of 1.9 in Gemma and 1.3 in Llama, suggesting that non-semantic weight structures play a crucial role. We show that steering vectors can be applied to the teacher to produce subliminal data, in addition to prompting and finetuning as used in previous studies. Analysis of the activations of the student models that have been trained on steered and prompted data demonstrates that students inherit not just the semantic meaning of the teacher's bias, but also the type of intervention that was used to apply it: steered students imitate steering vectors, prompted students do not. Additionally, the gradients of steered subliminal data show a linear correlation with the teacher's steering vectors, showing promise for data auditing. More broadly, as synthetic data becomes central to frontier training pipelines, being able to see the latent signals hidden in training data becomes paramount.

</details>


### [221] [When Do Prompt-Side Agent Playbooks Transfer? Accuracy, Cost, and Runtime Shift in Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05778)
*Weihong Lin,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了冻结的提示侧剧本书（prompt-side playbooks）在工具使用型语言智能体中的迁移性，通过蒸馏-验证-迁移协议在三个基准上评估。结果表明，冻结迁移是一种有条件的冷启动选项，而非默认复用策略或普遍优于目标侧再蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示侧剧本书无需重新训练即可改进工具使用型语言智能体，但其在源设置之外的便携性尚不明确。本研究旨在系统评估冻结剧本书迁移的可行性与局限性。

Method: 采用统一的蒸馏-验证-迁移协议，在ALFWorld、TAU2-Bench和XBench-DeepSearch三个基准上测试冻结剧本书的迁移效果。ALFWorld中使用受控贪婪解码和预算匹配比较；TAU2-Bench使用预指定的聚合对比并应用Holm校正；XBench-DeepSearch评估推理时迁移的代价与效益。

Result: ALFWorld上迁移有益，预算匹配下蒸馏指导优于五个固定演示。TAU2-Bench上聚合对比显示适度平均优势，但Holm校正后仅保留135个路径级效应中的一个，其余证据表明存在兼容性敏感的异质性。XBench-DeepSearch中一个工件-运行时配对保留了有用启发式，但导致重复查询、延迟停止和成本膨胀。

Conclusion: 冻结迁移是一种有条件的冷启动选项，而非默认复用策略或普遍优于目标侧再蒸馏。迁移和目标侧派生的剧本书均需要目标侧验证成功、终止、协议兼容性和成本。

Abstract: Prompt-side playbooks can improve tool-using language agents without retraining, but their portability beyond the source setting is unclear. We study frozen playbook transfer under a shared distill--validate--transfer protocol. On ALFWorld, transfer is beneficial under controlled greedy decoding and, in one near-budget-matched comparison, distilled guidance outperforms five fixed demonstrations. On TAU2-Bench, a prespecified aggregate contrast supports a modest average matched-domain advantage, but global Holm correction retains only one of 135 route-level effects; the remaining grid provides descriptive evidence of compatibility-sensitive heterogeneity. On XBench-DeepSearch, one artifact--runtime pairing preserves useful first-try heuristics while producing repeated queries, delayed stopping, and substantial cost inflation after a context-runtime shift. Across benchmarks, transferred and target-derived playbooks both require target-side validation of success, termination, protocol compatibility, and cost. Frozen transfer is therefore a conditional cold-start option, not a reuse-by-default strategy or a universally preferable alternative to target-side redistillation.

</details>


### [222] [Activity Frames: Deterministic Screen-Activity Compilation for Agent Memory and Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05784)
*Nossa Iyamu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性、零模型的编译管线，将被动捕获的屏幕活动（用户行为而非用户言语）转化为代理记忆，大幅压缩并提升问答准确率；同时作为需求侧成本工具，首次测量了例行开销比R和例行重复率h，揭示了当前计算机使用代理在记忆和成本效率上的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机使用代理在推理时需从头推导用户已执行过的例行操作，因为代理的记忆只记录用户说了什么，而非用户做了什么。这种“全前沿推理”导致巨大的计算浪费。作者希望利用被动捕获的屏幕活动（用户实际行为）来构建代理记忆，从而避免重复推理，并测量代理成本模型中的关键参数。

Method: 作者开发了一个确定性、零模型的编译管线：将本地捕获的屏幕活动流分割为类型化的活动帧（typed activity frames），再组合为有边界的片段（bounded episodes），附带应用、站点、时间、输入量及指向原始行的证据指针。整个流程无模型参与，输出字节一致、可缓存、可机械审计。该编译器还可作为需求侧成本工具，通过读取被动（委托前）的人类活动而非代理部署，来提供例行开销比R和例行重复率h两个参数。

Result: 在一位专业人士的128,756帧（51个活跃日）语料上，编译器将一天原始捕获压缩为提示就绪的上下文块，缩小86倍，耗时68毫秒。代理阅读该块后回答日常问题的准确率达98.4%（Wilson 95% CI 91.7-99.7%），而LLM摘要的准确率仅为66-80%。编译器测得R值为60-343倍，可委托的例行重复率h在样本内为9.0%，样本外为7.7%，全舰队令牌上限约为8%。

Conclusion: 通过将用户行为而非语言记录纳入代理记忆，可以大幅降低推理成本并提高问答准确性。编译后的例行操作可确定性重放（模型不参与），已在防护匹配攻击上实现零模型令牌的实时演示。该方案为构建更高效、可审计的计算机使用代理提供了新方向，且所有组件均已开源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents pay full frontier inference to re-derive routines their user has already performed, because an agent's memory today records what the user said, not what the user did. We compile passively captured screen activity into agent memory with a deterministic, zero-model pipeline: it segments a local capture stream into typed activity frames, bounded episodes carrying application, site, timing, input volume, and evidence pointers back to the raw rows, with no model in the loop, so the output is byte-identical, cacheable, and mechanically auditable. On one professional's single-user corpus of 128,756 frames over 51 active days, the compiler reduces a day of raw capture to a prompt-ready context block 86x smaller in 68 ms, and an agent reading that block answers questions about the day at 98.4% accuracy (Wilson 95% CI 91.7-99.7%) against an independent oracle, versus 66-80% for an LLM summary of the same capture, a mid-tier model reading the block matching a frontier one.
  The same compiler doubles as a demand-side cost instrument. Read off passive, pre-delegation human activity rather than agent rollouts, it supplies two parameters that agent-cost models assume but, to our knowledge, have not measured: the Routine Overhead Ratio R and the routine recurrence h. We report first values of R, a modeled upper bound, at 60-343x, and a delegable recurrence of 9.0% in-sample and 7.7% out-of-sample, for a realistic all-fleet token ceiling near 8%; a compiled routine replays deterministically with the model out of the loop, demonstrated live at zero model tokens on a guard-matched hit. Schema, compiler, and evaluation harness are open.

</details>


### [223] [ChainClaw: A Layered Agent Framework for Reliable On-Chain Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05790)
*Jiacheng Wei,Zhaoxin Fan,Xin Wen,Yuqin Lan,Dongrun Li,Wenjun Wu,Faguo Wu,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChainClaw is a blockchain-native agent framework that addresses three fundamental gaps (Reactivity, Irreversibility, Observability) in deploying LLM agents on-chain. It uses a layered architecture with event-driven orchestration, simulation-based safety, and on-chain monitoring, outperforming baselines on safety and task completion.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: General-purpose LLM agents fail in blockchain environments due to stateful, adversarial, and economically irreversible execution. Existing tool-augmented agents overlook on-chain specific constraints, leading to Reactivity (poor event handling), Irreversibility (no undoing actions), and Observability (limited state visibility) gaps.

Method: ChainClaw is built on OpenClaw with a layered architecture: (1) event-driven orchestration layer for reactivity, (2) simulation-based safety intelligence layer with pre-execution safety pipeline (transaction simulation + action guard) for irreversibility, (3) on-chain monitoring runtime layer with read adapter and transaction monitor for observability, all unified by a cross-layer memory subsystem.

Result: Evaluated on a purpose-built benchmark covering seven tasks across four categories and five dimensions. ChainClaw consistently outperforms representative baselines on both safety and task completion metrics.

Conclusion: ChainClaw effectively closes the three identified gaps, demonstrating that a blockchain-native design is necessary and feasible for safe and effective LLM agent operation in adversarial on-chain environments.

Abstract: General-purpose large language model agents have achieved strong performance on tool-augmented tasks, yet they rely on assumptions break down in blockchain environments. On-chain execution is stateful, adversarial, and economically irreversible, exposing three fundamental gaps: Reactivity, Irreversibility, and Observability. We propose ChainClaw, a blockchain-native agent framework built on OpenClaw, that addresses all three gaps through a layered architecture comprising an event-driven orchestration layer, a simulation-based safety intelligence layer, and an on-chain monitoring runtime layer, unified by a cross-layer memory subsystem. ChainClaw closes the Reactivity gap via event ingestion and simulation feedback, the Irreversibility gap via a pre-execution safety pipeline with transaction simulation and action guard, and the Observability gap via an on-chain read adapter and transaction monitor. We evaluate ChainClaw on a purpose-built benchmark covering seven tasks across four categories and five dimensions. ChainClaw consistently outperforms representative baselines on both safety and task completion.

</details>


### [224] [When Agentic AI Meets Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05792)
*Kai Li,Conggai Li,Sarah Ali Siddiqui,Syed Sohail Ahmed,Xin Yuan,Shenghong Li,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AISAC（Agentic Integrated Sensing and Communication）范式，将智能体AI与通感一体化系统结合，建立了一个包含观察、情境化、推理与预测、规划与编排、执行与协作、反馈与恢复的六阶段闭环框架，并引入五个智能体成熟度等级。通过对多模态智能、大语言模型、强化学习、联邦学习、RIS辅助控制、无人机与车辆网络、AI原生网络管理等领域的文献综述，发现现有研究在九个智能体特定评估标准中最多仅满足一或两个，揭示了宣称与展示的智能体成熟度之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于基于学习的感知、资源分配、可重构智能表面、边缘智能、多智能体协调和弹性网络的研究大多孤立发展，缺乏统一视角。亟需将这些分散的工作整合到目标驱动的闭环智能系统中，推动通感一体化从面向功能的物理层技术向智能体化范式转变。

Method: 提出了一个六阶段闭环框架（观察→情境化→推理与预测→规划与编排→执行与协作→反馈与恢复）和五个智能体成熟度等级。基于此框架，对多模态智能、大语言模型、强化学习、联邦学习、RIS、UAV与车辆网络、AI原生网络管理等领域的文献进行系统综述，并设计了九个智能体特定评估标准对代表性研究进行审计。

Result: 对代表性研究按照九个智能体特定评估标准进行审计，发现没有任何系统报告超过一或两个标准，这表明宣称的智能体成熟度与实际展示之间存在显著差距。现有系统尚未达到完全闭环的智能体通感一体化水平。

Conclusion: 开放挑战包括物理到语义的锚定、预测性世界模型、实时智能体与物理层交互、安全工具使用、异构多智能体协作、基准测试和资源高效自主性。AISAC范式为未来通感一体化系统向自主、目标驱动的闭环智能系统演进提供了统一的理论框架和发展路线图。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) from a function-oriented physical-layer technology into a goal-driven, closed-loop intelligent system, a paradigm we term AISAC. Existing work on learning-based sensing, resource allocation, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS), edge intelligence, multi-agent coordination, and resilient networking has developed largely in isolation. This survey unifies the literature within a six-stage closed-loop framework comprising observation, contextualization, reasoning and prediction, planning and orchestration, execution and collaboration, and feedback and resilience. It also introduces five levels of agentic maturity, ranging from physical-layer primitives to fully closed-loop agentic ISAC. We use this framework to review advances in multimodal intelligence, large language models, reinforcement learning, federated learning, RIS-assisted control, Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) and vehicular networks, and AI-native network management, and analyze privacy, security, resilience, and sustainability as cross-cutting requirements of the full perception-reasoning-action loop. An audit of representative studies against nine agentic-specific evaluation criteria shows that no system reports more than one or two of them, exposing a gap between claimed and demonstrated agentic maturity. We identify open challenges in physical-to-semantic grounding, predictive world models, real-time agent-PHY interaction, safe tool use, heterogeneous multi-agent collaboration, benchmarking, and resource-efficient autonomy.

</details>


### [225] [When Self-Evolution Backfires: Pre-Commit Gating against Skill Contamination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05810)
*Linfang Shang,Ming Xu,Yiding Sun,Tianle Xia,Lingxiang Hu,Lan Xu,Ning Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Self-evolving agents suffer from capability contamination when skill pools grow beyond a critical size, due to irreversible cross-round contamination chains. Verifier-as-Gatekeeper (VaG) uses three heterogeneous critics (structural validity, behavioral harmlessness, semantic consistency) plus marginal-gain subset selection to filter skills, achieving 72% pass@1 with a 5x smaller pool and positive transfer to other backbones/benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self-evolving agents accumulate skills from their own trajectories, but the process is not monotonic: beyond a critical pool size, adding new skills degrades performance. The paper aims to understand this degradation, identify its structural cause (cross-round contamination chains that are irreversible), and propose a solution to enable monotonic improvement.

Method: The paper formalizes the capability-contamination phase transition and shows contamination is structurally irreversible (post-hoc rollback recovers little performance). It proposes Verifier-as-Gatekeeper (VaG): a progressive trust hierarchy with three heterogeneous critics—structural validity, behavioral harmlessness, and semantic consistency—that individually filter each skill, plus a marginal-gain subset selection that removes combinatorial contamination before skills enter runtime context.

Result: On Terminal-Bench 2, unconditional accumulation peaks then degrades, with post-hoc removal recovering only a small fraction of the drop (empirical signature of irreversibility). VaG improves every round, reaching 72% pass@1 with a pool roughly 5x smaller. The frozen VaG skill pool transfers positively to four other backbones and a second benchmark without re-evolution. Ablations confirm the three critics are complementary and non-substitutable, intercepting largely disjoint harmful skill classes.

Conclusion: Skill admission in self-evolving agents must be a pre-commit necessity rather than a post-hoc fix due to structural irreversibility of contamination. VaG provides a viable approach for monotonic capability accumulation through multi-faceted verification and subset selection.

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate capability by distilling reusable skills from their execution trajectories, but we find this process is not monotonic: past a critical pool size, newly added skills degrade performance instead of improving it. We formalize this capability-contamination phase transition and trace it to a structural cause: once a defective skill enters the decision context, it becomes reference material for distilling later skills, forming cross-round contamination chains. We further show the contamination is structurally irreversible: removing a source skill after the fact cannot erase the flawed reasoning its descendants have already inherited, so post-hoc rollback recovers only a small fraction of the lost performance. This makes skill admission a pre-commit necessity rather than a post-hoc fix, and motivates Verifier-as-Gatekeeper (VaG): a progressive trust hierarchy whose three heterogeneous critics - structural validity, behavioral harmlessness, and semantic consistency - filter each skill individually, coupled with a marginal-gain subset selection that removes combinatorial contamination at the top tier before skills reach the runtime context. On Terminal-Bench 2, unconditional accumulation rises to a peak and then degrades, giving back most of its gains as the pool keeps growing, and post-hoc removal of the culprit skills recovers only a small part of the drop - the empirical signature of irreversibility. In contrast, VaG improves every round, reaching 72% pass@1 with a pool roughly 5x smaller, and its frozen skill pool transfers positively to four other backbones and a second benchmark without re-evolution. Ablations confirm the three critics are complementary and mutually non-substitutable, each intercepting a largely disjoint class of harmful skills.

</details>


### [226] [Cautious Context Steering for Language Model Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05813)
*Gihoon Kim,Jeyoung Lee,Suhan Woo,Sekwon Oh,Minsu Jeon,Hyounsoo Han,Euntai Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cautious Context Steering (CCS)，一种通过轻量级适配器在每步解码中动态决定用户上下文影响程度的语言模型个性化方法，仅需单数据集训练即可在域内和跨域泛化，同时避免每用户微调并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化方法（如每用户训练适配器或学习依赖用户的奖励模型）面临数据稀疏、对新用户和领域泛化能力差的问题；而 ICL 和 CoS 虽无需每用户训练，但 ICL 无法控制上下文影响，CoS 需固定系数且增加额外前向计算。因此需要一种既能动态控制上下文影响又高效的方法。

Method: CCS 在冻结的骨干 LM 上添加轻量级适配器，该适配器在每步解码时决定是否以及以多强程度将用户上下文融入生成。适配器从 oracle 上下文条件 LM 中学习这种行为，当上下文无帮助时保持基础 LM 不变。

Result: 仅单数据集训练的 CCS 适配器在域内和四个域外个性化基准上均提升了生成质量，展示了强泛化能力；同时避免了每用户微调和 CoS 所需的额外上下文条件前向传播，显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: CCS 通过轻量级适配器动态调控上下文影响，在无需每用户训练和降低推理成本的前提下，实现了对已知和未知用户及领域的鲁棒泛化，是一种高效且泛化性强的语言模型个性化方法。

Abstract: Personalizing language models (LMs) to individual user preferences is essential for aligning responses with diverse goals and backgrounds. Existing methods typically train a separate adapter for each user or learn a reward model whose scores depend on the user. Despite explicitly optimizing for each user, these methods must learn from limited observations and therefore suffer from data sparsity and poor generalization to unseen users and domains. In-context learning (ICL) and Context Steering (CoS) can instead provide more effective personalization by conditioning the base LM directly on user context and leveraging its pretrained capabilities without per-user training. Yet neither adapts the influence of that context across decoding steps: ICL leaves it uncontrolled, whereas CoS applies a fixed steering coefficient and requires two LM forward passes per step. We propose Cautious Context Steering (CCS), which adds a lightweight adapter to a frozen backbone LM to decide at each token whether and how strongly user context should affect generation. The adapter learns this behavior from an oracle context-conditioned LM and preserves the base LM when the context is not helpful. A single CCS adapter trained on only one dataset improves generation quality both in-domain and across four out-of-distribution personalization benchmarks, demonstrating robust generalization to new users and domains. CCS also avoids per-user fine-tuning and the additional context-conditioned forward pass required by CoS, substantially reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [227] [ViSR-KGC: Visual Subgraph Reasoning with Vision-Language Models for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05833)
*Jiafan Li,Mengxue Yang,Jiaqi Zhu,Liang Chang,Ying Li,Hongan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ViSR-KGC的视觉子图推理方法，用于多模态知识图谱补全（MMKGC）。该方法结合表示学习、视觉语言模型（VLM）和预训练模型的常识知识，将查询相关的子图转换为可视化图像，并与实体图像、文本描述及候选答案组成统一提示，让VLM推断缺失实体，从而融合结构拓扑、多模态证据与常识推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于嵌入的MMKGC方法在关系特定证据有限时效果不佳；而基于LLM的推理方法将图结构线性化为文本提示，丢失了结构拓扑和视觉信息；VLM虽然擅长多模态推理，但无法原生处理知识图谱中节点和边的复杂语义结构。现有方法难以同时兼顾图结构、多模态信息和常识知识。

Method: ViSR-KGC框架包含三个互补能力：通过表示学习捕获全局拓扑依赖、利用VLM分析局部多模态证据、以及利用预训练模型中的常识知识。首先基于多模态嵌入提取紧凑且查询感知的子图；然后通过经验比较选择的布局策略将子图可视化；最后将可视化子图、实体图像、文本描述和候选答案组合为统一提示，输入VLM以推断缺失实体。

Result: 通过将子图可视化并融合多模态信息，使得VLM能够有效处理知识图谱补全任务，同时保留了结构拓扑、视觉和文本证据以及常识推理能力。

Conclusion: ViSR-KGC弥合了传统嵌入方法、LLM线性化推理与VLM多模态能力之间的差距，为多模态知识图谱补全提供了一种融合结构、视觉和常识的推理范式。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion (KGC) aims to infer missing entities or relations from incomplete graph structures, and has evolved into multimodal knowledge graph completion (MMKGC), where entities are associated with multiple modalities such as text and images. Traditional representation learning approaches follow the embedding-based paradigm and may struggle when relation-specific evidence is limited. Meanwhile, LLM-based reasoning methods typically linearize graph structures into textual prompts, which obscures structural topology and neglects vital visual information. While vision-language models (VLMs) excel at multimodal reasoning, they cannot natively interpret structured graph topology, particularly when it comes to knowledge graphs where nodes and edges carry complex semantics. To bridge this gap, we propose ViSR-KGC, a visual subgraph reasoning approach for KGC. It integrates three complementary capabilities to capture semantic correlations: identifying global topology dependencies via representation learning, analyzing local multimodal evidence using VLMs, and providing necessary commonsense knowledge inherent in pre-trained models. Based on learned multimodal embeddings, our framework first extracts a compact and query-aware subgraph from the MMKG. Then, this subgraph is transformed into a visually interpretable image using a layout strategy selected through empirical comparison.Finally, the visualized subgraph, entity images, textual descriptions, and candidate answers are combined into a unified prompt, enabling the VLM to infer the missing entity.

</details>


### [228] [Runtime Observability for Heterogeneous Attention Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05863)
*Fanzhe Wei,Li Liu,Ziyang Wang,Chenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种运行时可观测性契约（WitProbe），覆盖四种模型记忆类（隐式缓存、学习型稀疏选择器、循环状态、普通KV缓存），通过三个操作符实例化于六种模型配置（五种架构族），组合成可执行的请求级风险账本。契约将误差度量作为类型携带，仅在度量匹配时允许组合，通过两个证明过的桥接跨越度量，无法形式化验证的部分自动降级为经验性度量。系统在1240万次条目读取、八路并发、每请求预算和失败闭合身份归因下运行，保持风险预算零违规。应用于DeepSeek-V4堆栈时，通过机器裁决的辨别活动将静默损坏定位到精确的结构边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模型不再使用普通KV缓存，而采用隐式缓存、学习型稀疏选择器或循环状态等多种记忆形式，每种形式在压缩下以不同方式失效，缺乏统一的运行时可观测性框架来量化和管理记忆风险。

Method: 定义了一个覆盖四类记忆的运行时可观测性契约，包含三个操作符；实例化于六种配置（五种架构族）；将每阶段的界组合成请求级风险账本；契约携带误差度量作为类型（仅度量匹配时允许组合），通过两个证明过的桥接跨越度量，无法形式化验证的部分自动降级为经验性度量；采用融合常开探针在CUDA graphs内观察指定子层；通过机器裁决的辨别活动定位静默损坏。

Result: 在1240万次条目读取、八路并发、每请求预算和失败闭合身份归因下，账本量化了当今见证上的诚实权衡，零违规地保持风险预算。应用于DeepSeek-V4堆栈时，将静默损坏精确定位到精确的结构边界（在免驱逐、身份隔离的精确区，所有观察到的失败在驱逐或槽复用区）。机器裁决的辨别活动拒绝了两个作者自己混淆的推理。

Conclusion: 该系统能够在现代复杂模型记忆架构中提供统一的、可验证的运行时风险监控，每个声明被认证、部分认证或经验性度量，组合继承最弱层级，层级由机器决定。所有工件、守卫和Lean开发已开源，论文中每个数字均可通过一个命令从发布工件再生。

Abstract: Modern models no longer keep a plain KV cache: latent caches, learned sparse selectors and recurrent states each carry the model's memory in a different form, and each fails differently under compression. We give a runtime observability contract that covers all four memory classes with three operators, instantiate it on six model configurations across five architecture families, and compose the per-stage bounds into an executable request-level risk ledger. Contracts carry their error metric as a type -- composition is only defined when metrics match, and this check rejected our own first composed chain; the repaired chain crosses metrics through two proved bridges, and whatever no formal system can certify is measured instead, dropping the composed tier to empirical automatically: every claim is certified, partially certified, or empirical, composition inherits the weakest tier, and the tier is decided by the machine. Replayed over $12.4$M entry reads and run under eight-way concurrency with per-request budgets and fail-closed identity attribution, the ledger quantifies the honest trade-off on today's witness and holds its risk budget with zero violations. A fused always-on probe observes a declared one-layer subset under CUDA graphs inside the serving noise floor. Applied to a served DeepSeek-V4 stack with a packed compressed-KV prototype, the same machinery localizes a silent corruption to a precise structural boundary -- exact in the eviction-free, identity-isolated regime, with every observed failure in an eviction or slot-reuse regime -- through a machine-adjudicated discrimination campaign whose calculus rejected two of our own confounded inferences along the way. All artifacts, guards, and the Lean development are released at https://github.com/metask-ai/witprobe-attention-memory; every number in this paper regenerates from the shipped artifacts by one command.

</details>


### [229] [Seeing Is Not Deciding: Can Multimodal LLMs Act as Effective CEOs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05864)
*Yuyang Dai,Xueqing Peng,Yuxia Wang,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了C-SUITEBENCH，一个面向高管决策的多模态基准测试，包含五个决策任务和50个场景，评估了九种前沿模型在文本-only和 multimodal条件下的决策能力。实验发现多模态输入能提升基于证据的推理，但出现“多模态整合悖论”：视觉信息改善了风险预测和董事会汇报，却导致所有模型在资源分配任务上表现下降。消融实验表明问题源于信号拥挤，即多种视觉通道组合干扰了约束满足。结论认为视觉感知与约束行动是可分离的瓶颈， indiscriminate 视觉增强可能损害高风险决策，需采用选择性融合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高管决策基准仅限于纯文本环境，无法评估模型是否能够感知视觉商业证据并有效整合以提升决策质量。因此需要构建一个受控的多模态基准来检验模型在视觉与文本信息结合下的决策能力。

Method: 设计C-SUITEBENCH基准，包含五个决策任务（风险预测、董事会汇报、资源分配等），在50个场景下分别提供纯文本和多模态（文本+视觉）两种条件。将九个前沿大语言模型（GPT-4、Claude等）置于CEO角色中，评估其决策能力。通过对比文本-only与多模态条件下的表现，并开展消融实验分析视觉通道的影响。

Result: 多模态输入一致提升了基于证据的推理，尤其在风险预测和董事会汇报任务上增益最大。但出现悖论：视觉信息在所有模型中导致资源分配任务（约束资源分配）的退化，尽管视觉接地能力本身有提升。消融实验揭示问题源于信号拥挤，单个视觉通道有益，但组合后破坏解码时的约束满足。

Conclusion: 视觉感知和约束行动是多模态智能体中可分离的瓶颈；不加选择的视觉增强可能损害高风险决策。未来高管AI系统应采用选择性融合策略，仅当视觉信息与决策约束兼容时引入。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly applied as autonomous decision-making agents. However, in executive business decisions, existing benchmarks are limited to textonly settings. This makes it unclear whether models can perceive visual business evidence and effectively integrate it to improve decision quality. We introduce C-SUITEBENCH, a controlled multimodal benchmark that includes five decision tasks under paired text-only and multimodal conditions across 50 scenarios. We place nine frontier models in the role of a chief executive officer and evaluate their decision-making ability. Multimodal inputs consistently improve evidence-centric reasoning, with the largest and most reliable gains appearing in risk forecasting and board-facing justification. However, we uncover a multimodal integration paradox: adding visual business information degrades constrained resource allocation for all nine models, even as visual grounding itself improves. Ablation experiments reveal that this failure emerges from signal crowding, although each visual channel helps individually, their combination disrupts constraint satisfaction during decoding. These findings demonstrate that visual perception and constrained action are separable bottlenecks in multimodal agents, and that indiscriminate visual augmentation can harm high-stakes decision making, motivating selective grounding strategies for future executive AI systems.

</details>


### [230] [Improving Interoperability among Defence and National Security Ontologies: Analysis and Evaluation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05867)
*Jonathon Dilworth,Pedro Giesteira Cotovio,David Herron,Paul Cripps,Nigel Dewdney,Catia Pesquita,Ernesto Jiménez-Ruiz*

Main category: cs.AI
