We further show that data generated by iARCS improves a base generator, supporting its value as a practical synthetic data generation tool rather than only a controllable scene editing method.

</details>


### [262] [MicroEvo: Knowledge-Guided LLM Sampling for Efficient Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06183)
*Jia Xiong,Runkai Li,Chenxu Niu,Guangyuan Gao,Changwen Xing,Yifan Zhang,Xinlai Wan,Jieran Cui,Chen Bai,Yusheng Hua,Ying Wang,Ming Ling,Xi Wang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MicroEvo将LLM与蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)相结合，用于多目标微架构优化，通过知识引导的框架在稀疏仿真预算下实现更优的帕累托前沿质量和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构设计空间探索面临搜索空间庞大和PPA评估成本高昂的问题，仅有少量仿真预算可用于设计决策。现有方法进行盲搜索，未考虑微架构依赖关系，也无法有效从迭代搜索中学习，导致评估浪费和帕累托收敛性差。

Method: MicroEvo框架包含四个关键组件：(1) LLM驱动的进化算子，利用大语言模型生成有意义的微架构变体；(2) 帕累托感知的树策略，平衡帕累托贡献和多样性；(3) 主动知识积累机制，提取和重用优化见解；(4) 状态感知指令，在线自适应调整搜索行为。

Result: 与NSGA-II相比，MicroEvo将帕累托前沿质量提升了最高36.2%，搜索效率提升了10.6倍，并且在复杂工业级核心上展示了强大的可扩展性。

Conclusion: MicroEvo通过知识引导的LLM+MCTS耦合框架，有效解决了微架构设计空间探索中的搜索效率和质量问题，为大规模工业级微架构优化提供了一种可行方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Microarchitecture design space exploration suffers from expansive search spaces and expensive PPA evaluation, leaving only a small simulation budget for design decision-making. Existing methods perform blind search without considering microarchitectural dependencies and fail to learn from the iterative search effectively, leading to wasted evaluations and weak Pareto convergence. In this paper, we propose MicroEvo, a knowledge-guided framework that couples off-the-shelf LLMs with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for multi-objective microarchitecture optimization. MicroEvo combines LLM-driven evolutionary operators, a Pareto-aware tree policy that balances Pareto contribution and diversity, an active knowledge accumulation mechanism that extracts and reuses optimization insights, and state-aware directives that adapt the search behavior online. Experiments show that MicroEvo improves Pareto-front quality by up to 36.2% over NSGA-II and achieves 10.6x higher search efficiency, and also demonstrates strong scalability to a complex industrial-scale core. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/GEAR-SEU/MicroEvo-ICCAD-26.

</details>


### [263] [Comparative Approaches to Agent Retrieval over Large Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06196)
*Indivara Kolluru,Nathan Sportsman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型技能库中智能体如何选择和排序技能的问题，对比了混合排序器（词法+稠密嵌入检索）与类型化知识图谱（编码工作流关系）两种方法。在117个真实查询上，混合排序器在前五名中命中正确技能的概率为73.5%，而知识图谱显著更差（-11.2个百分点），且图谱的边大多来自排序器已检索到的邻居，无法扩展检索范围。作者揭示了添加结构未能提升强排序器性能的机制原因，并指出使用作者编写的查询会高估命中率多达44个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型技能库中的智能体需要高效选择并排序相关技能，但将整个库加载到上下文中成本高昂且缺乏自主排序结构。现有方法（如知识图谱）试图通过引入工作流关系来提升检索，但其有效性缺乏深入分析。本文旨在系统性比较混合排序器与知识图谱的性能，并探究结构信息为何有时不能改善检索。

Method: 基于690个技能的语料库，设计了两种系统：1）混合排序器（结合词法检索与稠密嵌入检索）用于稀疏按需加载；2）类型化知识图谱（编码前提条件、数据流和顺序等关系）。在117个真实查询上评估，对比命中率（hit@5）。分析图谱边的来源及其与排序器检索结果的重叠程度。

Result: 混合排序器在top5中命中正确技能的比例为73.5%±8.0%，知识图谱显著更差（低11.2个百分点，p=0.0007）。图谱中LLM生成的边层并未优于通过局部嵌入传递免费获得的邻居；73%排序器未命中的查询在图谱中无法到达。98.6%的图谱边连接的是排序器已共同检索到的技能，表明图谱无法扩展检索覆盖范围。使用作者编写的查询会高估hit@5多达44个百分点。

Conclusion: 添加结构化知识图谱并不能超越强排序器的检索性能，因为图谱的候选边来自排序器已搜索的相同嵌入邻域，无法扩大召回范围。图谱虽能丰富关系语义，但无助于扩展检索覆盖面。研究揭示了添加结构依赖在何种条件下才是最优的，并强调了使用真实查询而非作者编写查询进行评测的重要性。

Abstract: Agents backed by large skill libraries must decide which skills to load and in what order. Loading the entire library into context is expensive and provides no structure for autonomous sequencing. We study two systems for this problem over a corpus of 690 skills: a hybrid ranker combining lexical and dense-embedding retrieval for sparse, on-demand loading, and a typed knowledge graph encoding workflow relations such as prerequisites, data flow, and ordering. On a set of 117 realistic, non-echoing queries, the hybrid ranker retrieves the correct skill within the top five in 73.5% +/- 8.0 of cases, leaving roughly a quarter of queries unserved. When used as the design intended (substituting graph neighbours for additional ranked results at matched token budget), the graph is significantly worse (-11.2 points, p = 0.0007). Its LLM-generated edge layer adds nothing over neighbours obtained free from a local embedding pass, and 73% of the queries the ranker misses are not reachable through the graph at all. We attribute this to a pre-filter topology bound. Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [264] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE 提出了一种名为"世界排演"(World Rehearsal)的强化学习方法，让 LLM 智能体在训练时通过自身扮演环境来生成工具调用的响应，从而无需依赖真实或合成执行环境，实现了高效的长时域工具使用训练，并在多个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练 LLM 智能体进行长时域工具使用通常需要与真实或合成可执行环境交互，但这些环境的构建和验证成本高昂，外部模拟器也难以接地。因此，本文旨在探索一种无需外部环境交互的智能体训练新范式。

Method: EnvACE 是一种智能体强化学习方法，其核心是"世界排演"(World Rehearsal)：策略在行动和排演之间交替——首先生成工具调用，然后扮演环境角色产生该动作引发的响应，后续决策依赖于排演得到的响应。策略的两个角色（智能体和环境）通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化。通过世界排演，策略将动作与环境响应之间的关系内化到参数中，形成一个能够直接支持决策的智能体世界模型。

Result: 在 BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench 和 FinMCP-Bench 四个基准上，EnvACE 取得了强劲且可迁移的性能，在整体评估中优于环境缩放基线。控制实验表明世界排演在多个模型规模上持续提升策略学习效果。在测试时，内化的世界模型允许智能体在正式执行前进行私有排演，在适度的排演预算下无需额外外部交互即可获得进一步收益。

Conclusion: 世界排演(World Rehearsal)为突破外部环境约束、规模化训练 LLM 智能体开辟了一条新路径。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [265] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TS-RAG是一种将检索增强生成（RAG）应用于时间序列预测的新方法，通过设计专门的参考令牌来融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息，在多个真实世界基准上取得了一致的先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RAG在大型语言模型中通过引入外部相关信息有效提升了能力，但在时间序列预测领域应用有限；现有时间序列模型受限于训练数据少、参数规模小、缺乏大模型的生成能力，直接拼接参考序列效果不佳。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，设计了专门的参考令牌（reference tokens）来有效融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列的信息，从而更稳健地捕获复杂的时间动态。

Result: 在多个真实世界的时间序列预测基准上取得了持续的最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: TS-RAG通过引入RAG机制并设计参考令牌，成功提升了时间序列预测的准确性，为该领域提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [266] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DASH (Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons) improves on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) for LLM reasoning by adaptively weighting token-level supervision based on the temporal evolution of divergences between teacher and student, achieving gains without extra forward passes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard OPSD provides dense token-level supervision but assigns equal weight to all local divergences, ignoring the temporal context (e.g., divergence history) during autoregressive generation. This under-exploitation of rollout structure limits adaptation of supervision weights.

Method: DASH computes the gap between local distillation signal and sequence-level mean, maps it to an adaptive propagation gate, and uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation, thereby adjusting token-level weights according to how local divergences evolve.

Result: On three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales, DASH consistently improves over matched vanilla OPSD reruns, with no additional teacher or student forward passes.

Conclusion: DASH effectively addresses the temporal structure limitation of OPSD, providing a simple yet effective enhancement for RLVR-based reasoning improvement.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [267] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何改进合成临床基准数据集，使其在保持下游实用检查通过的前提下更真实，特别是在隐私敏感的医疗领域。作者将基准修订形式化为效用约束下的真实性改进，并在一个护理缺口基准上验证，通过确定性修订提升了真实性指标（如缺失结构、可操作性等），同时保持效用不变，表明合成基准质量应显式优化，效用只是约束而非真实性的充分证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成临床基准数据集虽然能通过现有的效用检查，但在结构上仍不够真实，尤其在医疗等隐私敏感领域，运营数据难以获取。因此需要在不破坏现有下游效用检查的前提下，提升基准的真实性。

Method: 将基准修订形式化为效用约束下的真实性改进问题，要求数据集修改在提升真实性的同时保持高于某个运营效用底线。在基于Synthea生成的护理缺口基准上实例化，通过缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性和人群对齐四个维度衡量真实性，并采用两种确定性修订策略（与一种天真的密集化对照比较）进行改进。

Result: 基线基准非常薄弱：采样对缺失率79.44%，仅12.75%的行可操作，38.94%的患者无可操作指标，前三令牌集中度达100%。两种确定性修订在保持效用底线的同时改善了这些指标，而天真的密集化对照保留了不真实的模板化。此外，内部基准真实性与对聚合运营参考的源保真度是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准的质量应被显式优化，效用仅应作为一个约束条件，而非真实性的充分证据。研究建议在构建合成基准时，直接针对真实性指标进行优化，同时确保满足下游效用要求。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [268] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果审计框架（CauAudit），通过政策、轨迹、步骤三层干预分析多模态大模型中的视觉工具使用（如裁剪放大）是否真正影响答案。发现尽管存在整体准确率提升，但视觉工具使用经常缺乏因果有效性，存在"不调用而看"（返回观察无因果效应）和"看而不规划"（观察有信息但调用不连贯）两种失败模式，揭示了视觉工具使用的幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"思考+图像"范式让多模态大模型使用主动视觉操作（如裁剪放大），但这些操作往往只有边际或负向收益，且消耗更多token。模型可能重复裁剪无关区域，在直接推理能正确回答的问题上失败。作者希望探究返回的视觉证据是否因果地影响了答案，从而诊断工具使用是否真正有效。

Method: 将视觉工具使用形式化为因果图，区分观察介导路径和行为诱导捷径。通过三层干预审计：政策层（比较工具使用与直接推理）、轨迹层（在展开过程中破坏所有观察）、步骤层（在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察）。步骤层估计量Visual Evidence Gain（VEG）隔离每个返回观察的贡献。在6个代表模型和5个细粒度感知基准上实验。

Result: 发现政策校准错误存在两种失败模式：Calling Without Looking（返回观察对答案无因果效应）和Looking Without Planning（观察有信息但调用时序不连贯）。轨迹层诊断分解政策层准确率增益，显示增益集中在"校准少数"上。表明尽管有整体准确率增益，视觉工具使用在广泛展开中并不因果有效。

Conclusion: 视觉工具使用的整体准确率提升是误导性的，存在"幻觉"现象。提出因果审计框架可有效诊断视觉工具使用的真实有效性，推动更可靠的视觉推理系统设计。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [269] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QuanTiMedAI框架，结合智能体大语言模型（agentic LLM）与紧凑型量子递归网络进行心脏骤停患者死亡预测。在MIMIC-IV队列上，仅用605个参数实现AUROC=0.852，超出现有最优基线约2.9%，表明量子增强序列模型能以更少参数超越经典递归网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏骤停死亡预测依赖入院早期的静态摘要，忽略了ICU住院期间生理恶化与恢复的时间动态。需要一种能捕捉时间序列信息并高效建模的方法。

Method: 采用双组件架构：(1) 智能体大语言模型（agentic LLM）进行临床知情特征发现与选择；(2) 紧凑型量子递归网络（quantum recurrent network）进行时序感知的死亡预测。通过量子非线性特征增强实现低参数量的高效建模。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上，QuanTiMedAI的量子增强架构以仅605个参数达到AUROC=0.852，比当前最优基线提升约2.9%。消融实验系统验证了每个设计选择的贡献。

Conclusion: 量子增强的序列建模能以远少于经典递归网络的参数取得更优性能，为ICU死亡预测提供了一种高效、轻量级的新范式。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [270] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将概念激活向量（CAV）方法扩展到基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的多模态语音-文本评分系统，用于分析自动口语评估系统的公平性和可解释性。同时探索稀疏自编码器（SAE）在复杂神经嵌入空间中提供更清晰概念方向的能力。结果表明，概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于模型表示和架构，而非概念本身；对概念的敏感性也依赖于架构；SAE使概念更线性可恢复但削弱了原始激活空间的敏感性。研究强调了在审计偏差时需要区分概念可恢复性与概念影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险场景中用于给第二语言学习者的口语测试打分，因此必须证明其分数取决于口语水平而非无关的说话者属性（如母语或年龄）。基于Transformer的模型提高了评分准确性，但其黑盒表示使得公平性和可解释性分析更加困难。先前工作使用CAV检测基于特征的评分器中的偏差，本研究将此方法扩展到神经评分系统，以更好地理解模型内部表示与评分决策之间的关系。

Method: 将概念激活向量（CAV）方法应用于两种神经口语评估系统：基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器。CAV将人类可解释的概念表示为模型激活空间中的方向，通过梯度敏感性指标区分概念是否被编码在内部表示中以及是否影响预测分数。同时引入稀疏自编码器（SAE），在稀疏潜在空间中学习CAV并将其映射回激活空间，以处理复杂神经嵌入空间中线性可分性不足的问题。

Result: 概念的可恢复性主要取决于被探测的表示和架构，而非概念本身。对概念的敏感性也是架构相关的。SAE使概念在潜在空间中更线性可恢复，但会削弱原始激活空间中的敏感性，尤其是在低维层中。

Conclusion: 在审计口语评估系统的偏差时，必须区分概念可恢复性（概念是否被模型编码）与概念影响力（概念是否影响评分），两者不可混为一谈。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [271] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体系统在长轨迹和多错误场景下的关键错误检测难题，提出了错误生命周期追踪框架TrajDebug（通过多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别定位最早关键错误，并追踪错误解决状态和最终影响进行关键归因）和包含486个手动标注失败轨迹的基准TrajErrBench，实验表明TrajDebug在多个智能体基准上取得最佳性能，其诊断能为下游智能体改进提供可操作反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂任务中表现出色，但存在级联错误和调试困难的问题。关键错误检测旨在定位失败轨迹中导致最终失败的最早错误步骤，但面临两大挑战：长轨迹使得错误判断的证据分散在远处指令、观察和先前上下文中；且失败轨迹常包含多个局部错误，只有部分对最终失败负责。现有方法难以有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出TrajDebug框架，包含多粒度历史压缩（压缩长轨迹上下文）和基于证据的错误识别（利用分散证据判断每一步是否有误），并通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和最终影响来实现关键归因（区分哪些错误是最终失败的根本原因）。同时构建了TrajErrBench基准，包含486个来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的手动标注失败轨迹，涵盖工具使用和编程场景。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上，TrajDebug相比现有基线取得了最佳整体性能。应用研究进一步表明，TrajDebug的诊断能为下游智能体提供可操作的反馈，从而提升其成功率。

Conclusion: TrajDebug通过错误生命周期追踪有效解决了长轨迹和多错误场景下的关键错误检测问题，提升了智能体系统的可调试性。代码和数据将开源以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [272] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出可解释人工智能（XAI）在评估不足方面的局限性，通过DetoxAI图像识别系统（用于偏差检测和概念遗忘）加以说明；随后呈现了一个人类基础评估的图像分类解释方法实例，并探讨了适应带有概念漂移的演化数据流的解释方法，讨论了将反事实解释适配于此问题的经验；最后联系到追踪数据、模型和解释共同演化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI方法缺乏充分的评估机制，尤其在实际应用（如偏差检测、概念遗忘）中评估不足；同时，数据流中的概念漂移使得静态解释方法不适用，需要适应演化的数据环境；此外，数据、模型与解释三者共同演化的问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 以DetoxAI图像识别系统为案例进行偏差检测和概念遗忘的说明；采用人类基础评估（human-grounded evaluation）方法评估图像分类解释方法；探索针对概念漂移数据流的自适应解释方法，特别是适配反事实解释（counterfactuals）的经验。

Result: 展示了XAI评估不足的具体表现（通过DetoxAI案例）；给出了人类基础评估在图像分类解释中的应用实例；报告了反事实解释在概念漂移数据流中的适配经验；揭示了数据、模型与解释共同演化带来的挑战。

Conclusion: XAI需要更充分的评估框架；解释方法应具备对演化数据流（含概念漂移）的适应性；数据、模型和解释的协同演化是未来XAI研究的重要方向。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [273] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可执行事件轨迹的参数量化分析方法，用于视频中的事件计数任务。通过在三个受控视频任务（弹球墙壁接触、视觉闪烁、类别状态转换）中变化事件数量和频率，发现模型在时间推理上存在阶段性失效：对持续性状态转换可靠计数上限约12个事件，但对瞬态闪烁事件无法可靠计数；高计数高频率下仅0.2%最终计数正确，仅恢复18.1%真实事件。增加采样率虽提升最终分数但不保证事件序列忠实恢复。该方法将视频评估从聚合准确率转向细粒度诊断时间推理失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实视频基准虽覆盖广泛但固定片段混杂事件计数、速率、时长和视觉复杂度，难以隔离失败模式；程序化基准虽更好控制但仅评分最终答案而非审计报告事件与可执行真值的一致性。为此引入基于轨迹的参数量化分析以桥接这一差距。

Method: 在三个受控视频任务（弹球墙壁接触、视觉闪烁、类别状态转换）中生成2,190个视频，变化事件数量N和频率F，同时保持渲染固定。每个视频包含可执行事件轨迹，用于能力曲面估计和时间戳级评估。使用Gemini 3.6 Flash模型进行测试，并探索不同提示策略和采样率的影响。

Result: 在80%可靠性阈值下，模型对持续性状态转换可靠计数达12个事件（0.5和1.0 Hz），但对瞬态闪烁事件无可靠正计数区域。高计数高频率下仅0.2%最终计数正确，恢复18.1%真实事件。增加采样率将弹球准确率从19.6%提升至29.3%，但序列与真值一致仅3.7%。不同提示策略增益有限，真实视频评估同样集中在低事件计数成功。

Conclusion: 基于轨迹的参数量化分析将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转向细粒度诊断时间推理失败的具体位置和原因，揭示了事件表征、计数和频率对模型性能的阶段性影响。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [274] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS)，一个基于证据和评分准则的自动化心衰特征工程流水线，在500例虚拟患者记录上生成202个特征，将心衰表型分类AUROC从0.895提升至0.963（HFrEF）和从0.870提升至0.910（HFpEF），且特征在证据支持和方法学合理性方面达到81.5%的最高分，展示了自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）特征工程是临床研究和AI的主要瓶颈，占数据科学家工作量的39-45%，尤其在心力衰竭（影响约670万美国成人）领域需要整合碎片化EHR数据与疾病特定的、基于指南的临床推理。现有基于规则和大型语言模型的方法仅提供部分自动化，且可维护性和证据可追溯性有限。

Method: 开发了Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS)，这是一个证据关联、基于评分准则的自动化心衰特征工程流水线。在500个虚拟患者记录（来自9个EHR源表）上评估，生成了132个结构化和70个基于评分准则的聚合特征，并通过受限LLM验证了结构完整性、评分准则合规性和来源可追溯性。

Result: nMAS生成了202个特征（132结构化+70评分式）。聚合特征将HFrEF表型分类的held-out AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910。独立的基于LLM的评分准则评估（证据支持和方法学合理性）得分为最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 证明了自动化、可审计的特征工程在复杂心血管EHR数据中的可行性，但评估仅基于单一机构队列，需要外部验证。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-MLB 是一个基于全血 RNA 表达数据、用于区分多发性硬化症（MS）与健康对照的开放机器学习基准，在 GSE17048 队列上进行了多种算法的系统评估，其中梯度提升在保留测试集上取得最优性能（MS Research Score 93.83, AUC-ROC 0.989）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MS 的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI 及实验室证据等复杂流程，血 RNA 表达数据可能携带疾病相关免疫信号，但现有研究缺乏一个可重复、开放且泄漏控制的基准来公正比较不同机器学习模型在 MS 与健康对照分类任务上的表现。

Method: 利用公共 GSE17048 队列构建 MS 与健康对照的二分类任务；采用嵌套交叉验证、未经触碰的分层保留集、Bootstrap 置信区间、ROC 与精确率-召回率分析、校准度量以及一个探索性 MS Research Score 作为评估指标；在整个流程中严格控制数据泄漏，确保可复现；并设计了外部模型提交通道。

Result: 梯度提升模型在保留集上排名第一：MS Research Score 93.83、AUC-ROC 0.989、灵敏度 0.950、特异度 0.778、F₁ 分数 0.927、Brier 分数 0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB 是首个面向 GSE17048 全血 RNA 表达数据中 MS 与健康对照分类的开放基准，提供了标准化的评估框架和外部模型提交路径，但该评分仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [276] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTER是一种用于时间序列预测的修正性特征发现方法，它冻结预训练骨干网络，通过两种互补生成器（原始输入通道的组合搜索和LLM生成的特征代码）挖掘残差中的可解释特征，并用验证集驱动的门控机制筛选特征，再通过验证集选择的修正器应用这些特征或保持原预测不变。在6个公开数据集和6个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER优于所有专用特征工程系统，修正效果提升约一倍，最弱骨干误差降低达27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器经常以结构化、重复的方式失败，通过微调修复成本高昂。因此，需要一种轻量级的后处理修正方法，通过挖掘冻结模型残差中的可解释特征来驱动修正，而无需修改主干模型。

Method: CRAFTER方法包含两个互补的特征生成器：一是对原始输入通道进行组合搜索，二是利用大语言模型（LLM）提出命名特征组合、二进制标志和可执行代码片段。所有候选特征通过一个基于验证集的统一门控机制决定接受或拒绝，然后由验证集选择的修正器应用已接受的特征，或者保持预测不变。该管道与特征来源无关，允许在相同条件下评估先前的特征工程系统。

Result: 在6个公共数据集和6个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在每种特征预算下均超越所有专用特征工程系统，修正效果相比单独使用修正器提升约一倍，将最弱骨干网络的误差降低高达27%。这些改进在不同LLM后端下稳健，并且在微调后的骨干网络上仍然有效。

Conclusion: CRAFTER通过修正性特征发现有效改进了冻结预训练预测器的性能，其源无关管道不仅提供了更好的修正效果，还成为一个评估工具，能够将预测改进归因于特征来源本身。该方法在多个数据集和骨干网络上表现稳健，甚至适用于已微调的模型。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [277] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程基于大语言模型（LLM）流程的概率语言，能够量化并传播不确定性，同时允许在不额外添加代码的情况下实验不同的推理缩放技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建依赖LLM的可靠应用面临挑战，因为LLM的输出往往缺乏准确性和明确的置信度度量，这种不确定性在多个LLM调用和其他工具构成的流程中会进一步累积，使得开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 引入一种概率语言，使开发者能够量化并传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并且无需在流程逻辑之外添加任何代码即可实验不同的推理缩放技术。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该能力，并通过一个案例研究构建了一个用于Rocq定理证明器的定理证明智能体。

Conclusion: 该概率语言有助于构建更可靠的LLM应用，通过不确定性量化与传播来增强信任度。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [278] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CGTime is a 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model that decouples perception from description by using deterministic code to compute statistics from multivariate series and an LLM to verbalize them, outperforming larger models on multivariate understanding tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training multimodal models to align time series with language faces a self-supervision trap: label quality is capped by the LLM's perceptual skills, and most datasets use single variables while important patterns require multivariate data. Existing methods are either reliable, realistic, or scalable but cannot achieve all three.

Method: Decouple perception from description by using deterministic code to compute a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series, and then using an LLM to verbalize those precomputed facts. This produces CGTime, a 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model.

Result: CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks, attaining the best multivariate fact score (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

Conclusion: By decoupling perception (handled by computation) from expression (handled by LLM), the trilemma is resolved: the method is reliable, realistic, and scalable.

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [279] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与推理显式解耦，利用现成感知模型构建结构化3D证据，并通过LoRA微调LLM进行显式几何推理，在空间推理任务上达到竞争性能，同时提升可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过大规模联合训练隐式学习3D感知和推理，但这种方式缺乏可解释性且计算开销大。作者观察到现代感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，而LLM擅长组合式符号推理，因此希望利用两者互补优势，将感知与推理显式分离，探索更高效、可解释的空间推理范式。

Method: 提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)，包含两个阶段：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型（如分割、深度估计、位姿估计等）将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据（如物体列表、空间关系）；(2) 通过LoRA高效微调LLM，使其仅基于这些显式3D几何证据进行推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具调用策略。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上，DiSR无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略即达到竞争性性能，同时具备更优的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知和推理是空间智能中一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，相比于端到端建模具有更好的可解释性、模块化和效率优势。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [280] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，因子化生成模型中通过匹配潜变量风格（z_s）的边缘分布到高斯先验来声称其与类别信息独立是不正确的。边缘匹配无法约束条件分布，导致风格变量仍可高度预测标签。作者通过理论分解和实验证明，仅靠边缘分布统计无法验证独立性，并提出了部分缓解策略但仍存在泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战因子化生成模型中普遍存在的错误理解：通过将潜变量风格（z_s）的边缘分布正则化为高斯分布，就认为其与类别标签独立。作者指出这一解释有误，因为边缘分布匹配并不约束类条件分布，从而潜变量风格仍可能携带大量类别信息。

Method: 理论推导了因子化采样所需的四个条件，指出边缘分布匹配不足以保证独立性；实验上，使用一个案例模型和四个代表性潜变量基线模型，通过全局MMD、线性探针、聚类准确率、外部条件生成评估等指标测量信息泄露；并尝试了六种扰动和四种缓解策略（包括事后条件先验和经验风格库）。

Result: 案例模型和基线模型在全局MMD接近零时，线性探针仍能以74%-100%准确率（基准10%）恢复类别标签；模型聚类准确率达99.15%，而外部条件生成成功率仅16%。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。事后条件先验在MNIST上条件生成得分0.97，CIFAR-10上仅0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达0.88。

Conclusion: 没有任何仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度可以证明其与类别标签独立；仅报告边缘统计量无法验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的独立性属性。研究强调了因子化生成模型在评估和正则化方面的不足，并指出了正确的验证方向。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [281] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态指令遵循中的"评分标准理解"问题，提出PRISM数据合成框架（四阶段生成带优先级的评分规则及验证轨迹）和PRISM-Eval评估指标（基于确定性匹配的宽松/严格准确率）。仅用10K合成数据即可将Qwen3-VL-4B在严格准确率上从9.5%提升至30.1%，且保持通用基准性能，该增益可迁移至其他开源多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态指令通常包含多个重要性不等的要求，但大多数多模态训练数据仍将指令遵循简化为回答一个自包含的问题，缺乏对多规则、优先级感知的建模。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：生成人物-任务对、前缀引导的规则集、质量过滤的评分标准、结构化验证轨迹。同时提出PRISM-Eval评估方法，采用宽松和严格两种确定性匹配指标，无需推理时的评判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的严格准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时在通用基准上保持平均性能；该增益可迁移至另外四种开源多模态大模型（包括密集和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化的评分标准监督是一种可扩展的路径，能够实现多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [282] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一个参数高效的微调框架，通过将多任务监督微调中的过拟合感知状态压缩为可合并的适配器状态，避免了存储完整模型检查点的高成本。它冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器，在任务峰值时回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余活跃任务上训练。采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层作用于低秩权重因子，更新保持线性、秩受限且可精确合并到冻结投影中。在五个骨干网络上平均准确率达到61.36%，优于msft的59.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务监督微调（SFT）通常将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务达到最佳泛化能力的时间不同。msft通过任务级展开、排除和回滚解决了这一不匹配问题，但其原始实现将调度器状态物化为完整模型检查点，使得阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署方面成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT将过拟合感知的多任务SFT的携带状态重新表示为紧凑、可合并的适配器状态。具体方法：冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器；在任务级峰值时回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余活跃任务上训练。在层级别，每个适配器采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层作用于低秩权重因子（而非样本表示），使得更新在输入上保持线性、秩受限，并可以精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干网络的比较协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达到61.36%，而对应的msft参考行为59.85%。AuroSFT在所有五个骨干网络上均获得更高的准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过参数高效的适配器状态表示和可合并的更新机制，有效降低了多任务SFT中阶段转换的存储和部署成本，同时提升了模型性能，证明了其方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [283] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroOFT 是一种改进的量化正交微调方法，通过在 QOFT 的线性正交变换旁添加零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支，在保持量化兼容性和初始状态等价性的同时，引入输入依赖的非线性校正能力，在 Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B 模型上相比 QOFT 提升 1.30-2.70% Macro-6 指标，相比 QLoRA 提升 6.52-10.62% 并节省 32.3-44.7% 可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOFT 虽能实现低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新局限于线性正交变换，缺乏输入依赖的非线性校正能力，限制了模型表达能力。

Method: AuroOFT 在 QOFT 的稳定量化兼容分支基础上，为每个适配线性层附加一个零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支。该分支将激活映射到 RMS 归一化的紧凑隐空间，使用自适应非线性基与有界/令牌依赖的门控机制。零初始化的上投影使得 AuroOFT 在初始化时与 QOFT 功能等价，正交性作为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT 在 Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B 设置上相比匹配的 QOFT 提升 Macro-6 指标 1.30-2.70%，超过 QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模下相对于 QLoRA 节省 32.3-44.7% 的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT 通过引入零初始化的非线性残差分支有效增强了 QOFT 的表达能力，在保持量化友好和参数高效的同时取得了显著的性能提升，代码已公开在匿名仓库。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [284] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向个人投资者的、全栈集成的税收感知投资组合管理系统。系统基于FastAPI后端和Web前端，通过三阶段强化学习架构（自监督跨资产编码器、混合专家分配策略与学习型意图路由器、LoRA个性化适配器）将用户自然语言投资目标转化为实时、对接券商API的投资组合推荐。系统功能完整并通过端到端集成测试，但尚未面向真实用户开放；作者同时报告了14天滚动回测（含自助法置信区间）作为部署前验证，以及若干工程实践教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人投资者缺乏机构客户享有的个性化、税收感知的投资组合管理服务：现有机器人顾问采用静态规则分配，机构级系统又设有账户门槛和技术要求。本文旨在填补这一空白，为零售投资者提供可及的、智能化的投资组合管理工具。

Method: 采用三阶段强化学习系统：(1) 自监督跨资产编码器，学习资产间的深层关系；(2) 基于混合专家（MoE）的分配策略，内含可学习的意图路由器，将用户自然语言描述的投资目标路由至六种投资授权之一；(3) 轻量级LoRA适配器，通过观察用户个人券商行为数据个性化推荐，无需重新训练共享模型。系统后端为FastAPI，前端为Web仪表板，集成Alpaca券商API（纸交易模式），包含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计动作完整性链。

Result: 系统在功能上完整并通过了端到端集成测试，包括与真实券商API的对接、多用户认证、预览确认流程等。提供了14天滚动回测（含自助法置信区间）作为初步的部署前验证。报告了若干工程实践教训：静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端实证验证相对于信任检查点元数据的价值。

Conclusion: 这是一个处于部署前阶段的新兴应用，有明确的完整部署路径。作者诚实地报告了其现状和初步验证结果，认为从该系统获得的工程经验对于构建基于外部实时数据源的强化学习应用具有普适的参考价值。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [285] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）在预测山火后泥石流中的表现，结合SHAP可解释性分析和TabPFN生成合成数据增强，发现TabPFN在未增强数据上表现最优（威胁指数0.637），短期降雨强度和风暴累积量是关键特征，合成数据增强能有效提升多数模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山火后泥石流对社区和基础设施构成严重威胁，但可靠的机器学习模型构建面临三大挑战：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间重叠、模型可解释性要求、训练数据有限。现有缺乏对这些挑战的系统性解决方案。

Method: 基于美国西部流域尺度的山火后泥石流观测数据，比较15种模型（含TabPFN）；采用重复分层交叉验证评估性能；使用SHAP分析特征重要性；并利用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强，验证其对各类模型的影响。

Result: 未增强数据中TabPFN威胁指数最高（0.637），树模型紧随其后；SHAP显示短期降雨强度和风暴累积量最重要，燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较低；合成数据增强使除CNN外的所有模型性能提升，深度学习模型平均威胁指数提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 本研究通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释特征分析和合成数据增强，为改进山火后泥石流预测提供了综合框架，证实了TabPFN在此任务中的优势及数据增强的有效性。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [286] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OSPDIM框架，一种基于黎曼流形的源自由在线无监督域适应方法，通过信息最大化在线修正几何偏差，解决EEG-BCI中标签偏移导致的分布失配问题，在多个运动想象数据集上显著优于标准黎曼方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）隐式假设类别先验平衡，但在实际在线BCI场景中标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术几何上失配目标数据分布，影响性能。

Method: 提出OSPDIM框架，在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，通过在线信息最大化优化该参数以纠正由不平衡数据流导致的几何偏移，实现无需全局批统计的实时校正。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵仿真中可视化验证OSPDIM能纠正标准中心化无法处理的失配；在多个运动想象数据集上，OSPDIM在在线适应场景中显著优于标准黎曼基线方法，尤其在严重类别不平衡条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际可插拔的BCI系统提供了鲁棒的在线域适应解决方案，有效处理标签偏移问题，具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [287] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QEvict 是一种三层 KV 缓存管理方案，将传统不可逆的 token 驱逐替换为可恢复的驱逐（保留高置信度窗口的完整精度，中间窗口量化存储并可解量化提升，仅删除最低置信度窗口），从而缓解自回归 LLM 推理中 KV 缓存的内存压力。通过引入 Future Missed Mass 和 Global LIR 两个诊断指标，QEvict 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上优于传统的驱逐和量化基线，减少了未来被遗漏的注意力并提高了信息保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 KV 缓存驱逐策略基于注意力分数做出不可逆的删除决策，但解码过程中 token 和窗口的重要性会动态漂移，导致原本被驱逐的 token 可能在后续步骤中再次变得重要，从而永久丢失有用信息。

Method: QEvict 采用三层缓存结构：1) 全精度高置信度窗口；2) 量化可恢复的中间窗口（可解量化并提升至全精度）；3) 仅删除最低置信度窗口。解码时通过累积注意力分数动态更新窗口重要性，量化窗口若重新变得重要则被解量化并提升。

Result: 在固定内存预算下，QEvict 保留了更广泛的历史上下文，同时为最重要的区域保持精确的全精度。在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上，QEvict 一致优于代表性的驱逐和量化基线，减少了未来被遗漏的注意力并改善了信息保留。

Conclusion: 可恢复的驱逐策略比不可逆的二元删除更有效地应对解码过程中的重要性漂移，为 KV 缓存管理提供了一种更灵活、性能更优的范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [288] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse，一种面向异构客户端联邦学习的重用规则。服务器重用最旧可用的本地模型更新，通过可配置的最大重用年龄限制陈旧度，并设置硬缓存年龄限制和最小新鲜客户端配额，新鲜更新采用自适应每张量Top-K表示。在六个图像分类数据集上，50个虚拟客户端，Dirichlet标签异质性α=0.5，90轮预算下，实现83.36-85.42%的上行链路节省，而匹配的Top-K FedAvg为76.88%；精度差异-5.29至-0.14个百分点。最佳测试精度相对匹配FedAvg为-2.38至+0.45个百分点。考虑下行链路对称影响后，节省降至41.68-42.71%，增量增益为3.24-4.27个百分点。该研究仅在模拟器中表征了所提规则，并未建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛或优于现有陈旧更新和懒惰聚合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中通信开销是主要瓶颈，特别是在异构客户端场景下。现有方法如Top-K压缩可以减少通信量，但仍有改进空间。本文探索通过重用陈旧更新来进一步降低上行链路通信量，同时保持模型精度。

Method: 提出DG-FedReuse规则：服务器根据当前全局模型版本确定重用资格，重用最旧的合格本地模型更新；设置最大重用年龄(MaxReuseAge)限制陈旧度；设置硬缓存年龄限制(HardCacheAgeLimit)和最小新鲜客户端配额(MinFreshClientQuota)来约束重用；新鲜更新采用自适应每张量Top-K数值场表示（即根据张量自适应选择Top-K系数）。

Result: 在六个图像分类数据集上，50个虚拟客户端，Dirichlet α=0.5，三颗随机种子，90轮预算。DG-FedReuse的上行链路节省为83.36-85.42%，匹配的Top-K FedAvg为76.88%；种子对齐的精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。测试控制检查点下的最佳测试精度相对匹配FedAvg为-2.38至+0.45个百分点。考虑对称密集模型下行链路敏感性后，头条节省降至41.68-42.71%，增量增益为3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究在实现的模拟器中表征了所提重用规则；并未建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛或优于现有陈旧更新和懒惰聚合方法。结果表明通信结论依赖于核算边界，下行链路成本不可忽略。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [289] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了量子结构化世界模型（QSWM），一种受量子理论启发的预测世界建模框架，使用复值表示和密度矩阵式潜在状态，并在基本元胞自动机上测试了其局部预测能力和长程推理局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子理论中的数学结构（如复值表示、密度矩阵）是否为世界建模提供有益的归纳偏置，以改进传统基于向量、概率分布或循环网络的世界模型。

Method: 提出QSWM框架，包含结构化潜在状态、潜转移算子和测量式解码映射；建立了经典包含性、预测充分性和结构化紧凑性三个基础性质；实例化了复值型和密度矩阵型两种变体。

Result: 在基本元胞自动机任务上，复值QSWM在局部预测上表现有竞争力，但长程回滚效果不佳；密度矩阵型变体在各类评估中均存在明显局限性。

Conclusion: 量子启发的复值表示在局部预测中具有一定潜力，但在长程推理和密度矩阵变体方面仍待改进，提示未来需要更有效的量子诱导偏置设计。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [290] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可证明的pipeline，通过凸方法（OSF）学习隐式谱预测器，再蒸馏为显式线性状态空间模型（LDS），从而端到端地从非线性动力学系统中提取紧凑的线性表示，避免了非凸系统辨识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统的学习通常涉及非凸优化，难以保证全局最优。作者希望绕过直接求解非凸系统辨识问题，通过凸学习方法获得与最优线性观测器竞争的预测器，并进一步蒸馏为显式的线性动力学系统，从而提供理论保证。

Method: 首先使用观测谱滤波（OSF）——一种凸方法——从非线性系统的观测中学习隐式谱预测器；然后通过谱到LDS蒸馏（spectral-to-LDS distillation）将该预测器转换为显式的循环线性动力学系统（LDS）。

Result: 主要定理表明，蒸馏后的LDS的平均预测误差分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由最优观测器的Luenberger复杂度控制的OSF学习项；该保证是维度无关的，仅依赖于观测器复杂度而非非线性系统的潜在维度。实验在线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆上验证了该pipeline产生的紧凑LDS预测器匹配或优于直接训练的基线。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是第一个端到端可证明的方法，通过凸学习 followed by 可证明的蒸馏，从非线性动力学中提取最佳事后线性状态空间表示。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [291] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需群体标注即可识别受虚假相关性影响的样本的方法。通过训练收敛后对输入施加固定扰动，观察预测翻转情况：符合虚假相关性的样本分类规则共享，更鲁棒；不符合的样本分类更脆弱，易翻转。利用检测到的样本重新平衡训练，显著提升最差群体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖早期训练信号识别受虚假相关性影响的样本，需要选择干预的 epoch，该超参数通常需要群体标注的验证数据。本文旨在无需群体标注、无需早停的情况下，利用收敛后的信号识别此类样本。

Method: 在模型收敛后，对每个训练样本输入施加固定扰动（如小噪声或对抗性扰动），进行两次前向传播。比较扰动前后的预测结果：若预测翻转，则认为该样本属于脆弱样本（即不符合虚假相关性的子群体）；若预测不变，则认为该样本符合虚假相关性。随后，使用检测到的脆弱样本（或同时使用两类样本）重新平衡训练，例如上采样或重加权。

Result: 在 Waterbirds 数据集上，最差群体准确率从 ERM 的 57.3% 提升至 80.8%，接近使用真实群体标签的 85.8%。过程仅需每个训练样本两次前向传播，无需任何群体标注或早停。

Conclusion: 收敛后模型的输入扰动响应可用于区分符合和不符合虚假相关性的样本，且无需群体标注。该方法简单有效，为在缺乏群体标签时提升模型对稀有子群体的鲁棒性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [292] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFlowNets），将生成流网络框架（AFlowNets）扩展到不完全信息博弈中，证明原有约束不完全信息博弈中无效，提出新泛化形式，实验表明IFlowNets在三个标准博弈环境中性能与速度均优于或持平于OSMCCFR和标准RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前将强化学习与反事实遗憾方法结合的算法较多，但生成式采样框架在不完全信息博弈中的应用研究较少。本文旨在将生成流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈场景。

Method: 提出信息流网络（Information Flow Networks, IFNs），作为对抗流网络（AFlowNets）在不完全信息博弈中的推广。证明了原有AFlowNets的约束条件在不完全信息博弈中无法获得合法密度（即玩家策略）和有效训练目标，并给出新的泛化约束，使IFlowNets严格推广AFlowNets。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境中，IFlowNets在性能和速度上均与OSMCCFR（Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret）和标准RL方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets严格泛化了AFlowNets，能够有效处理不完全信息博弈，并在初步实验中展现出良好的性能和效率。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [293] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生成训练中"探索"（Explorative Modeling, XM）的第三轴本质是"自由度"（freedom），即模型行为所隐含约束的弱化程度。理论证明平均XM损失取决于候选输出错过可接受区域的概率，且探索将该概率提升至K次幂；实验表明XM优化的其实是自由度，而非传统意义上的生成表现力。在分布偏移下，直接选择自由度能显著提升XM性能（30例中胜出29例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成训练中每个比较仅产生一个输出，导致能输出常见答案的模型反而优于保留更广泛可能性的模型。Explorative Modeling (XM)通过每个比较生成K个输出并选择最接近的进行更新，声称探索是"第三预训练轴"，与生成表现力相关。本文旨在澄清这一轴的真正本质，并证明其实际上是自由度，即模型行为所隐含约束的弱化程度。

Method: 理论部分：推导平均XM损失与候选输出遗漏可接受区域概率的关系，证明探索将遗漏概率提升至K次幂，且对K>1，匹配概率随自由度增加。实验部分：设计Forward XM实验，测量不同K值下的自由度变化；训练XM候选池，将验证集选择方法与基于无标注父上下文读取的自由度选择器进行对比（30个案例）。

Result: Forward XM实验中，更大的K增加或饱和了测量的自由度，且在上下文依赖目标下每个测试值都增加了自由度。在30个案例的比较中，自由度选择器在29例中胜出。此外，自由度选择在归纳实验中比最小描述长度（MDL）方法性能提升110-500%。

Conclusion: 生成表现力只是自由度的模式计数代理，丢弃了自由度具有泛化意义的扩展结构。XM是手段，自由度是目的。选择自由度能在分布偏移下改进XM性能。自由度是函数属性而非形式属性，参数、架构、MDL和数据可变化而行为约束不变，且最弱的模型最可能泛化。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [294] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvoHarness-RL，一种通过学习离线 harness 策略（管理外部工作空间的状态构建与运行时访问）来增强长程 LLM 智能体的方法。在 ALFWorld 上使用 Qwen3-8B 达到 96.9% 的成功率，并揭示了 harness annealing（策略内化）和 harness evolution（状态精简）两种关键动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程 LLM 智能体依赖外部执行支持（harness）来维护状态、跟踪进度、调用工具等，但 harness 的设计和使用策略通常依赖人工工程（提示、启发式、领域惯例），缺乏系统化的学习机制。状态形成（从噪声交互轨迹中构建）和运行时控制（外部状态访问）两个核心问题尚未得到联合解决。

Method: EvoHarness-RL 将 Belief、Progress、Experience (BPE) 作为策略可感知的 harness 状态。采用两阶段训练：1) Supervised Harness Fine-Tuning (SFT) 教会基座智能体 harness 动作空间和如何构建有用外部状态；2) Cost-Aware GRPO 探索协调策略，在长程交互中选择性读取、更新和整合该状态。在 ALFWorld 上使用 Qwen3-8B 实例化。

Result: EvoHarness-RL 在 ALFWorld 上达到 96.9% 的成功率，并揭示两种关键动态：1) Harness Annealing：训练将重复的 harness 使用模式内化到模型策略中，使智能体从频繁调用 harness 转向选择性外部状态访问；2) Harness Evolution：进度更新和经验整合将 harness 精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基底。
